世界経済：AI関連需要の広がりが世界景気をけん引。企業の景況感は堅調に推移
 製造業は節目50を上回る良好な水準を維持。非製造業は金融・消費関連を中心に加速
― 製造業はAI関連投資がけん引し、防衛支出の拡大も寄与。一方、中東情勢を受けた在庫積み増しの寄与は縮小傾向
― 非製造業は金融サービスやAI関連需要がけん引。エネルギー高で落ち込んでいた観光・娯楽も回復
 国・地域別では、米国では非製造業が大幅に改善。日本やユーロ圏では製造業を中心に改善

― 日本ではAI・半導体需要と外需拡大がけん引し、ユーロ圏は電気・電子部品を含む中間財の生産が好調。AI投資ブー
ムの広がりがうかがわれる
1
製造業の景況感（購買担当者景気指数） 非製造業の景況感（購買担当者景気指数）
（注） 日米欧：S&P Global、中：国家統計局。購買担当者景気指数：企業に対するアン
ケート調査結果から作成した指数。新規受注や生産高、雇用、出荷遅延などの指数
に一定のウエイトを掛けて算出。50が景気拡大・縮小の分岐点
（出所）S&P Global、中国国家統計局より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
（注） 日米欧：S&P Global、中：国家統計局。購買担当者景気指数（非製造業）：企業に対
するアンケート調査結果から作成した指数。50が景気拡大・縮小の分岐点
（出所）S&P Global、 中国国家統計局より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

### Page 3

金融市場概況：原油高を背景に日米金利が上昇
 日米株価は、9月下旬にハイテク株を中心に上昇
― 9月上旬は、日米株価とも軟調。原油高を背景とした日米金利上昇が重荷に
― 下旬に入ると、米AI関連株が買い戻されたことからナスダックが上昇に転じ、日経平均もつれ高の展開に
 ドル円は一時円高が進展したものの、日米金利差が拡大し、再度150円台後半まで円安が進む「往って来い」の展開に
― 日本・米国とも利上げを実施。先行きも利上げ継続見込みだが、米金利上昇幅がより大きく、日米金利差は拡大
― 日銀は物価上振れリスクに対応し、利上げペースを加速へ。次回利上げは26年12月会合と予想
2
（出所）LSEGより、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

米国：個人消費は一時的に減速も、サービス中心に底堅さ維持
 7月実質個人消費支出は前月比＋0.0％と減速も、一時要因による下押しが大きく、消費の基調は崩れていないと評価
― 例年より前倒しで6月に実施された大手通販セールの反動から、財消費が減少
― 一方、そうした影響を受けづらいサービス消費については強さを維持。高齢化を背景に医療支出が拡大したほか、W杯
開催等を受け、娯楽サービス、外食・宿泊、輸送サービスなどの選択的支出も増加
 小売販売の高頻度データは、足元で伸びが再び高まっており、7月の減速が一時的であったことを示唆

3
（出所） 米商務省より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

米国：所得減速下でも株高による資産効果が消費を下支え
 消費が底堅い背景に、富裕層を中心とした株価上昇による資産効果の持続
― 株高を受けてインフレ下でも家計の実質金融資産は着実に増加。可処分所得対比でも高水準を維持。実質所得が弱含
む中でも、金融資産の増加が家計の購買力を下支え
 先行きは物価鈍化に伴う実質所得の改善も消費の追い風に
― 実質可処分所得は前年比＋0.5％と伸び悩みが継続。先行きは物価鈍化を受けて実質所得も伸び幅を徐々に持ち直す
見込み
4
（出所） 米商務省より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

### Page 6

米国：雇用は予想外の大幅増。一時的要因の影響を受けつつも労働市場の底堅さを確認
 8月の非農業部門雇用者数は前月差＋16.2万人と市場予想（同＋5.6万人）を大幅に上回る結果
― 過去2カ月も合わせて5.5万人の上方修正。速報段階で減少した7月もプラス転化
― 娯楽・宿泊（同＋6.2万人）や教育・医療（同＋2.8万人）を中心に民間就業者が伸びを高めたほか、政府部門も同＋3.5
万人増と押し上げに寄与
― 政府部門では、バックトゥスクールに伴い教員、スクールバス運転士、カフェテリアスタッフなどの雇用が増加（例年みら
れる季節的な動きであるが、統計上の調整にゆがみが生じている可能性）
5
（出所）米労働省より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

（年初来変動幅、万人）
（年/月）
失業率は低位で横ばい
低失業率の一因に「少ない供給」
米国：失業率は低位安定。厳格化する移民政策が労働供給を一段と下押し
 失業率は4.1％と前月からほぼ横ばい。引き続きFOMC参加者が見込む長期均衡水準（Longer run）を下回る低水準
 低失業率の一因に労働参加率の低下。シニア層の退職増加に加え、移民政策の厳格化の影響も大
― 米移民・関税執行局（ICE）の摘発増など移民取り締まりが強化されるなか、メキシコ出身者などの労働人口は減少傾向
― 足元ではハイチなど複数国の一時保護資格（TPS、※）が相次ぎ終了。今後もエルサルバドルなどで有効期限を迎え、7

月以降の累計で最大100万人程度がTPSに基づく就労資格を失う可能性。労働供給を更に押し下げる要因に
（※）紛争や災害等により安全な帰国が困難な特定国の出身者に対し、一時的な在留・就労を認める制度
6
（出所）米労働省より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
失業率 移民の出身国別労働人口とTPS終了の対象国・規模
（注）TPS保有者数は各国について入手可能な最新値。ベネズエラは2023年指定分。9月以降のTPS終了
時期は現時点での有効期限であり、今後の延長・終了決定等により変更される可能性がある
（出所）米労働省、米移民評議会、米国土安全保障省、米議会調査局より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

米国：エネルギー高・関税でディスインフレは足踏み、期待インフレも高止まり
 エネルギー高や関税が重石となり、インフレ率の低下は足踏み
―8月のCPIが前年比＋3.4％と横ばいにとどまるなか、コアCPIも同＋2.4％（7月同＋2.5％）と減速は緩やか。サービス価
格は原油高を背景に航空運賃などが上昇、財価格には関税の影響が残存
 消費者の期待インフレ率は3％台で高止まり、2％目標への収束はなお見通しづらい状況
―FRB高官からは、5年以上に及ぶ目標超過で高インフレが定着するリスクを警戒する声も
7

や原油価格上昇の影響が大きいと考えられる品目を分類

欧州：米国と比べて鈍い消費回復ペース。実質金融資産の目減りが消費回復の重石に
8
 原油価格高騰一服を受けて、家計のマインドは底打ち
―7～9月期以降は下押し圧力が緩和する公算も、米国に比べて消費回復ペースの鈍さは否めず
 最悪期は脱しつつある一方で、実質賃金の伸び悩みは継続。高インフレによる金融資産の目減りも逆風に
―4～6月期のユーロ圏妥結賃金は前年比＋2.4％と前期（同＋2.6％）から鈍化。＋3％前後のインフレに割り負けている
状況変わらず。実質金融資産の目減りも相まって、家計の貯蓄率は高止まり
（出所）Eurostat 、米商務省より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

欧州：ドイツでは防衛・インフラ投資の効果が一部発現。恩恵は年後半以降一段と顕在化
9
 財政拡張を支えに、ドイツでは防衛・インフラ関連業種の生産・受注が拡大
― 生産水準が急速に高まる武器・弾薬の他、足元では鉄道車両の生産も拡大
― 公共部門によるインフラ投資の受注残は直近2四半期で＋10％超の増加
 今後の財政支出余地を踏まえれば、当該業種への恩恵は年後半以降、一段と顕在化する見込み
―6月末時点の執行額は既に2025年通年の約8割に達するも、年間予算に対する執行率は4割弱にとどまる

4～6月期
７月
（前期比、％）
欧州：4～6月期の輸出拡大はエネルギー市況や特需が主因。輸出競争力の低迷は変わらず
10
 エネルギー市況や石化製品の代替調達需要増等を受け、4～6月期は輸出が拡大
―4～6月期の輸出金額は前期比＋5.6％（輸出数量：同＋2.1%）の増加。単価上昇によりエネルギーが急増した他、アジ
ア系メーカーの生産調整を受けた代替調達需要の高まり等を背景に、化学製品が増加
 ただし、底流にある輸出競争力の低迷は変わらず。AI関連財の外需寄与はネットでマイナス
―AI関連財の輸入額は年初来＋27.1％増加し、輸出の伸び（同＋14.0％）を大きく上回る
EU：AI関連財の輸出入
（注）AI関連財はWTO の定義に基づき分類。EU域内の輸出入を除く

日本：耐久財特需一服後も、所得改善を背景に緩やかな回復基調を維持
16
 耐久財特需の押し上げは一服

― 一方、サービス消費は増加基調を維持。個人消費は一時的な上振れから元の緩やかな拡大トレンドへ回帰する動き

― 実質賃金の伸びは低下も、夏季賞与要因の剥落が原因。実質所定内給与は足元でも1％程度の伸びを維持
― 懸念材料は再び上昇し始めた原油価格。高インフレが再燃すれば、個人消費の回復に水を差す懸念

日本：日銀追加利上げは最短インターバル。今後も日銀は3カ月に1回程度のペースで利上げへ
17
 日銀は9月会合で利上げを決定（反対2名）。利上げは6月以来3カ月ぶりで、今次局面では最も短いインターバル
―9月会合後の会見で植田総裁は、物価押し上げから物価上振れ抑制へと「政策の局面が変化した」と説明
― 政策金利は1.25％と、日銀による中立金利推計値のレンジ下限を上回るが、金融環境は利上げ後も緩和的と評価
 今後も日銀は3カ月に1回程度のペースで利上げを続ける可能性大
― 大幅利上げや連続利上げについては物価上振れリスクが大きい場合の選択肢と説明。一方、急激な政策変更を回避す

る観点から、先手を打って利上げを実施していく姿勢
物価上振れリスクへの対応から
利上げ間隔が短期化
政策金利とブレークイーブン・インフレ
（注） ブレークイーブン・インフレは基調的物価上昇率の一面を捉えていると考えられるが、
必ずしも基調的物価上昇率そのものを示す指標ではない点に留意
（出所）日本銀行、LSEGより、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成 （出所） 日本銀行より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
日銀9月会合のポイント（声明文・総裁会見）
声明文
• 企業の賃金・価格設定行動の積極化や中長期的な予想物価上昇
率の上昇などを踏まえると、2％物価目標を上振れるリスクがある
• 企業間取引における価格上昇圧力が消費者段階にも波及し始め
ているほか、賃金上昇の販売価格への転嫁の動きも続いている
• 政策金利の変更後も、緩和的な金融環境は維持される
会見での注目発言
• 基調的物価上昇率が2％に接近し、政策局面が変化。物価上振れ
を回避する観点が重要に
• 原油価格の上昇やそこから波及した物価上昇圧力、AI関連需要

や円安進行が幅広い品目の物価上昇圧力に繋がる可能性
• 基調的物価の2％定着を確認するのにはしばらく時間がかかる
• 金融緩和の度合いは利上げにより少しずつ縮まっているが、銀行
貸し出しや資産市場は依然として緩和的
• 急激な利上げで金融環境が大きく変化することや、それによる経
済へのマイナス影響を回避する観点は重要
• 大幅利上げに追い込まれる可能性を減らすように政策を運営する

米国債券市場：インフレ懸念と追加利上げ観測を背景に利回りは上昇
 9月の米10年国債利回りは、インフレ懸念の高まりから5.2％まで上昇
― 月前半は、雇用統計の上振れや原油高、物価指標の高止まりから、長期金利は5％の大台に
― 中旬は、FOMCが25bpの利上げを決定。追加利上げの可能性も示唆され、長期金利は5％前後で高止まり
― 月後半は、堅調な景況感やFRB高官のタカ派発言を背景に、長期金利は5.2％台まで上昇
 今後は、ホルムズ海峡を巡る中東情勢の動向が鍵に。原油高が続けば、複数回の追加利上げにも現実味
― トランプ大統領がイランの提示した停戦案を拒否するなど、中東情勢の先行きは依然として不透明

国内債券・為替：長期金利は一時3.1％超え。ドル円は往って来いで円安継続へ
 長期金利は上昇。一時3.115％と1996年8月ぶりの水準に
― 日銀9月会合前後で利上げ前倒しを織り込む動き。米FRBの追加利上げ観測も、円安進行を通じて金利上昇の要因に
― 先行きも原油価格や米金利の高止まりが見込まれるもと、日本の長期金利も3％超での推移が続く可能性
 9月のドル円は往って来い。先行きも、150円台を中心とした円安水準が続く見込み
― 9月前半は、ベッセント財務長官の円安警戒発言や日銀利上げ観測の高まりを受け、一時152円台後半まで円高に
― もっとも、円高基調は定着せず。9月後半にかけては米利上げ観測の高まりを背景に150円台後半まで再度円安が進行

―WTI 原油価格は、サウジアラビアの主要パイプライン停止等から一時100ドル超へ急伸。パイプラインの部分的な稼働

1

國際經濟金融週報內容摘要 
美 
國 
美國經濟維持溫和擴張，勞動市場降溫與通膨黏著牽動聯準會政策 
經濟 
⚫ 2026 年第 2 季美國實質 GDP 季增年率終值上修至 2.2%， 惟低於第1 季
之 2.5%。成長主要由個人消費及固定投資帶動，其中消費貢獻 2.51 個
百分點；惟進口大增 12.6%，致淨出口拖累 1.10 個百分點。另實質私人
國內最終銷售成長 4.6%，顯示內需仍具韌性。 
⚫ 9 月美國非農就業僅增 2.9 萬人，遠低於前月及近一年平均，失業率升
至 4.2%， 薪資增幅續降 ， 顯示企業聘僱動能轉弱 。 惟勞動市場尚未明顯
惡化，後續仍須關注就業降溫對聯準會利率決策之影響。 
⚫ 8 月美國職位空缺數由 733.5 萬個降至 707.9 萬個，職缺率降至 4.3%，
顯示企業徵才需求略為降溫；惟聘僱人數增至 519.2 萬人。主動離職及
裁員規模同步下降，反映勞工轉職意願與企業人力調整趨於審慎，勞動
市場供需大致維持平衡。 
⚫ 9 月美國 ISM 製造業採購經理人指數為 54.5，連續第九個月維持擴張。
新訂單、就業及未完成訂單同步改善，顯示需求仍具韌性；惟生產、出
口及進口動能略為放緩，且原物料成本持續攀升，加上關稅政策、地緣
政治及供應鏈不確定性仍存，製造業仍面臨部分壓力。 
⚫ 8 月美國 PCE 物價指數月增 0.3%、年增 3.4%，核心 PCE 月增 0.2%、
年增 3.0%， 顯示通膨大致持穩但短期物價壓力略升 。 另一方面 ， 個人消
費支出月增 0.9%、實質消費成長 0.6%，帶動儲蓄率降至 4.1%，顯示家
庭需求仍具韌性；後續須關注消費熱度及通膨對聯準會政策之影響。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 近期聯準會官員立場趨於分歧，理事 Michael Barr 認為能源價格及 AI
投資熱潮可能延緩通膨降溫 ， 未來仍可能進一步升息 ； 惟John Williams、
Philip Jefferson 及 Michelle Bowman 等官員則主張暫無急於升息的必要 ，
傾向觀察後續經濟數據。加以 9 月非農就業明顯放緩，市場對 10 月升
息預期大幅降溫，焦點轉向 12 月會議。 
⚫ 台積電傳評估與馬斯克旗下 Terafab 合作赴德州設廠，雙方仍處討論階
段。合作可能由台積電主導營運，或由 Terafab 持多數股權、台積電提
供技術 。 若成真 ， 將擴大台積電美國布局 ， 並支援Tesla、SpaceX 及 xAI
晶片需求。

短評 
⚫ 美國經濟目前仍維持溫和擴張，內需與投資表現優於預期，短期衰退風
險有限 ； 惟近期新增就業明顯低於預期 ， 企業聘僱意願與職缺需求放緩 ，
勞動市場動能已有轉弱跡象，而通膨受能源價格、 AI 投資及關稅等因
素影響仍具黏著性。在經濟成長放緩與通膨尚未完全受控的環境下，聯
準會短期可能傾向維持觀望，惟若通膨再度升溫，不排除年底前仍有進
一步升息空間。 
歐 
元 
區 
法國殖利率大幅上升，不利於投資及消費表現 
經濟

個百分點至 3.8%、2.5%。 
⚫ 歐元區 8 月失業率持平 6.4%。

⚫ 歐元區第二季房價年增率為 4%，較第一季降 0.6 個百分點。 
重要 
事件

125 個基點以上。 
短評 ⚫ 法國財政前景不明朗 ， 未來仍可能面臨殖利率進一步上升與財政壓力加
劇的風險，暫時不利於消費及投資表現。 
日 
本 
景氣溫和復甦，但消費放緩與通膨升溫風險升高 
經濟

短評

性，經濟可望維持溫和復甦，惟日本央行進一步升息的可能性正逐步提
高。 
中 
國 
為刺激內需、引導產業走向，人行漸近式放寬政策工具 
經濟 ⚫ 9 月官方製造業 PMI、非製造業 PMI 分別上升為 50.1、50.2。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 人行於 9 月 29 日宣布下調抵押補充貸款(該貸款本質是將資金引導至政
府希望發展的領域)利率自 1.75%降至 1.50%，並擴大適用範圍。 
短評 
⚫ 未來若內需復甦力道不如預期 ， 不排除人行將進一步降準或擴大結構性
貨幣工具支持經濟 ； 至於全面降息則仍須兼顧人民幣匯率及中美利差等
因素，可能性相對較低。

國際經濟金融週報 
兆豐國際商業銀行 徵信處 
115.10.5 
一、 美國—美國經濟維持溫和擴張，勞動市場降溫與通膨黏著牽動聯準會政策 
2026 年第二季美國實質 GDP 季增年率終值由前次估計之 1.5%上修至 2.2%，
惟仍低於第一季之 2.5%， 顯示經濟持續擴張但成長動能略有放緩 。 此次上修主要
反映投資、消費及政府支出優於原先估計。就成長結構觀察，個人消費支出仍為
主要動能，貢獻 2.51 個百分點，其中服務及商品消費分別貢獻 1.57 及 0.94 個百
分點；私人投資貢獻 0.82 個百分點，以設備投資及智慧財產權投資表現強勁，惟
庫存調整拖累 0.53 個百分點。對外貿易方面，出口成長 5.0%，但進口大幅增加
12.6%， 致淨出口拖累GDP 成長 1.10 個百分點 。 另反映內需基本面的實質私人國
內最終銷售成長 4.6%， 高於第一季之1.8%， 顯示民間需求明顯增強 。 物價方面 ，
第 2 季 PCE 及核心 PCE 物價指數分別上升 5.0%及 3.3%， 顯示通膨壓力仍偏高 。
整體而言，美國經濟仍由消費與固定投資支撐，惟進口快速增加及通膨黏著性，
仍為後續經濟前景的重要觀察重點。 
2026 年 9 月美國非農就業人數增加 2.9 萬人 ， 明顯低於上月修正後的13.3 萬
人，也低於過去 12 個月平均每月增加 4.5 萬人的水準；失業率小幅升至 4.2%，
整體仍維持在 3 月以來 4.1%至 4.3%的區間，反映勞動市場整體變化有限，惟新
增就業力道已轉趨疲弱。家庭調查方面，勞動參與率升至 61.8%，就業人口比為
59.2%，均較上月小幅上升；長期失業人數約 194.4 萬人，占總失業人口 27.1%，
大致維持穩定。產業別方面，主要產業就業變化有限，其中醫療保健增加 1.7 萬
人、營建業增加 1.1 萬人、製造業增加 0.9 萬人，金融活動則減少 0.7 萬人，政府
部門減少 1.7 萬人。薪資方面，私人部門平均時薪升至 37.81 美元，月增 0.1%、
年增 3.0%，平均每週工時維持 34.4 小時。另 7 月與 8 月新增就業合計下修 6 萬
人，反映前二月就業動能較初估疲弱。整體而言，9 月美國新增就業明顯放緩、
失業率小幅上升且薪資增速降溫，企業聘僱動能較先前轉弱；惟失業率仍維持於
近期區間，勞動市場尚未出現明顯惡化跡象，後續仍須關注企業聘僱需求是否持
續降溫，以及對聯準會利率決策的影響。 
2026 年 8 月美國職位空缺數(Job Openings) 由上月修正後之 733.5 萬個減少
25.6 萬個至 707.9 萬個 ， 職缺率由4.4%降至 4.3%， 顯示企業用人需求略有降溫 ；

### Page 6

本報告係根據研究資料及各項可靠數據作成，兆豐銀行保留報告內容所有權利，未經本行許可，請勿引
用相關內容。

5

惟聘僱人數由 514.6 萬人小幅增加至 519.2 萬人，聘僱率由 3.2%升至 3.3%。總離
職人數由 512.8 萬人減少至 507.0 萬人，離職率維持於 3.2%。就離職結構觀察，
主動離職(Quits)人數由 308.9 萬人降至 306.6 萬人，主動離職率維持 1.9%；裁員
與解僱(Layoffs and Discharges) 人數則由 170.2 萬人降至 164.1 萬人，裁員率由
1.1%降至 1.0%。產業別方面，職位空缺增加主要來自零售業、金融保險業、資訊
業及休閒餐飲業 ； 聘僱人數則以政府部門及製造業增幅較為明顯 。 整體而言 ，8 月
美國職缺數較前月下滑，反映企業徵才需求略有降溫，但聘僱活動仍維持穩定，
且主動離職與裁員規模同步下降，顯示勞工轉職意願及企業人力調整趨於審慎，
勞動市場供需仍大致維持平衡。 
2026 年 9 月美國 ISM 製造業採購經理人指數(Manufacturing PMI) 為 54.5，
較上月之 54.6 微降 0.1 點，惟已連續第九個月維持於 50 以上擴張區間，顯示製
造業景氣持續擴張。細項觀察，新訂單指數由53.7 升至 55.3，需求動能續增；生
產指數由 58.3 降至 56.7，雖仍維持擴張，但成長速度略有放緩；就業指數由51.2
升至 52.7，反映企業聘僱需求持續增加。供應商交貨指數由 59.3 微降至 59.0，交
貨延遲情況仍存；庫存指數則由 50.6 降至 48.6，企業備貨態度轉趨保守。此外，
價格指數由 71.1 升至 77.9，原物料及投入成本壓力升高；未完成訂單指數由51.8
升至 56.4，訂單去化變慢。另一方面，新出口訂單指數由53.2 降至 50.9，進口指
數亦由 52.5 降至 51.0，外需成長動能及企業備料需求略為放緩。整體而言，9 月
美國製造業活動持續穩健擴張，新訂單、就業及未完成訂單同步改善，顯示製造
業景氣仍具韌性；惟出口及進口增速放緩、投入成本壓力升高，加上關稅政策、
地緣政治風險及供應鏈不確定性仍存，後續發展仍值得持續關注。 
2026 年 8 月美國個人消費支出(PCE)物價指數月增率由上月之 0.1%升至 0.3%，
年增率維持於 3.4%； 扣除食品及能源後之核心PCE 月增率由 0.1%升至 0.2%，年
增率亦持平於 3.0%，顯示整體通膨大致持穩，惟短期物價漲勢較前月略有回升。
另一方面，個人所得月增 0.2%，可支配所得(DPI)月增 0.3%，實質可支配所得持
平；個人消費支出月增 0.9%，實質個人消費支出成長 0.6%，顯示民間消費表現
明顯回溫。由於消費增幅高於所得增幅，個人儲蓄率由前月之 4.6%降至 4.1%。
整體而言，8 月美國通膨表現大致持穩，但消費支出顯著回升，反映家庭需求仍
具韌性；後續仍須持續關注消費熱度、通膨走勢及其對聯準會貨幣政策路徑之影
響。

### Page 7

用相關內容。

6

近期聯準會官員談話呈現鷹鴿分歧。聯準會理事 Michael Barr 認為，中東局
勢推升能源價格，以及 AI 相關投資熱潮帶動需求增長，均可能阻礙通膨回落至
2%目標 ， 未來仍不排除進一步升息的必要性 ； 惟 紐約聯準銀行總裁 John Williams、
聯準會副主席 Philip Jefferson 及金融監理副主席 Michelle Bowman 則強調，在 9
月升息後，當前並無急迫性立即進一步收緊政策，應先觀察後續通膨、就業及金
融市場數據變化，再評估是否有進一步調整利率的必要。受官員談話轉趨審慎，
以及 9 月非農就業表現疲弱影響，市場對 10 月 FOMC 會議再度升息的預期明顯
降溫，政策焦點逐漸轉向 12 月會議及年底前是否仍有一次升息空間。 
晶圓代工龍頭台積電傳出正評估與馬斯克旗下 Terafab 合作，在美國德州布
局晶圓廠，馬斯克也於社群平台 X 證實雙方正在討論，並表示 「目前僅在討論階
段 ， 但可能會有進展」 。 目前浮上檯面的合作模式包括由台積電持有並營運新廠 ，
SpaceX 或 Terafab 透過入股及承諾採購晶圓參與；另一方案則由 Terafab 掌握多
數股權 ， 台積電提供製程技術與晶圓廠營運經驗 。Terafab 旨在因應 Tesla、SpaceX
及 xAI 快速成長的 AI 晶片需求，首期投資規模達 168 億美元。若合作成真，不
僅可望成為台積電繼亞利桑那州後在美國的新布局，也凸顯先進製程與穩定晶片
供應的重要性，但目前具體合作方式、時程與投資內容仍未正式定案。 
二、歐元區—法國殖利率大幅上升，不利於投資及消費表現 
 歐元區 9 月 HICP 通膨 、 核心通膨(不含食品及能源)分別上升 0.6、0.1 個百分
點至 3.8%、2.5%， 主因國際原油價格上漲 ， 使能源通膨上升4.5 個百分點至 18.8%，
並透過肥料、物流成本上漲，間接推升食品通膨 0.3 個百分點至 1.4%。另外，服
務通膨上升 0.2 個百分點至 3.2%，近三個月服務通膨大致呈現橫向波動，部分反
映旅遊旺季因素影響。基於布蘭特原油持續位於每桶 90 美元以上，預期年底前
仍將對通膨帶來額外壓力，後續須關注能源漲價可能會透過運輸、物流等成本管
道傳導至核心商品及服務價格，進而提高核心通膨黏性。 
 其他經濟數據包含 ：(1)歐元區 8 月失業率持平 6.4%， 失業人數增加2.6 萬人 ，
主要是義大利勞動參與人數增加所致，不過 ，基於PMI 顯示近期德國勞動雇用需
求持續增溫，將有助於穩定勞動市場表現；(2)歐元區第二季家庭儲蓄率下降 0.2
個百分點至 14.2%，顯示通膨壓力削弱民眾購買力，致儲蓄暫時性減少；(3)歐元
區第二季房價年增率為 4%， 較第一季降0.6 個百分點 ， 主因法國中古屋面臨嚴格

### Page 8

本報告係根據研究資料及各項可靠數據作成，兆豐銀行保留報告內容所有權利，未經本行許可，請勿引
用相關內容。

7

環保法規，須負擔高昂翻修成本，使法國房價呈現連三季下滑所拖累。 
 近一週法國 10 年期公債殖利率升至 4.9%以上，與德國公債殖利率差距擴大
至 125 個基點以上，主要反映市場對法國主權公債可持續性疑慮升溫。儘管目前
政府計畫將 2027 年財政赤字縮限至 5%，惟政府在國會並無穩定多數席次，即使
能以行政命令強行通過預算 ， 也可能面臨倒閣風險 ；此外 ，市場對於2027 年 4 月
總統大選後，新總統能否取得國會足夠支持，推動削減赤字與控制債務的改革亦
缺乏信心。因此未來法國仍可能面臨殖利率進一步上升與財政壓力加劇的風險，
暫時不利於投資及消費信心。 
三、日本—景氣溫和復甦，但消費放緩與通膨升溫風險升高 
 8 月工業生產月減 1.7%，主要係受到該月地震及豪雨等天然災害影響，導致
部分工廠暫時停產。從細項來看，汽車業受停工影響最為明顯，產量下滑6.8%，
電子產品產量亦減少 1.9%。 惟生產機械產量連續三個月成長 ， 加上核心資本財出
貨持續增加，顯示企業投資需求仍相當強勁，有助於支撐後續工業生產回升。從
企業預期來看，製造商預期9 月產量將成長 3.2%，顯示生產活動可望迅速回溫，
在供應鏈逐步恢復、庫存回補需求持續，以及穩健的企業資本支出支撐下，預計
第三季工業生產仍可維持正成長，且成長動能可望延續至年底。 
 8 月零售月減 1.2%，雖然表現略優於市場預期，但仍顯示民間消費動能正在
放緩。此次疲弱主要原因來自耐久財銷售下滑，其中家電銷售因空調等產品在換
機需求告一段落後明顯回落，汽車銷售也隨政府刺激措施效應逐漸消退而連續第
四個月下降。相較之下，食品、服裝等非耐久財銷售雖同步走弱，但降幅相對有
限 ， 反映薪資成長仍對消費提供部分支撐 。惟不利天候與通膨壓力仍是主要風險 ，
8 月至 9 月持續豪雨已影響服裝等非必需消費，而除米價以外的食品及商品價格
持續上漲，也正侵蝕家庭實質購買力，恐限制消費者信心與消費復甦力道。 
 9 月東京核心通膨年增率升至 2.7%，雖然東京都免費托兒及免費水費政策造
成的基期效應貢獻約 0.5 個百分點，但通膨升溫主因仍是價格上漲已由能源與食
品擴散至整體經濟。受石油價格上揚帶動，企業開始加速將原物料及薪資成本轉
嫁給消費者，帶動商品與服務價格普遍走高；租金通膨更升至 2%，創 1994 年以
來新高，公共交通及醫療等項目也開始調漲。隨著東京的物價壓力逐步反映至全
國數據，日本央行進一步升息的可能性升高。

四、中國—為刺激內需、引導產業走向，人行漸近式放寬政策工具 
9 月官方製造業 PMI 上升 0.3 點至 50.1，為近三個月高點，新訂單、出口訂
單指數分別為 50.5、50，並帶動生產指數上升至 51.7，不過，庫存指數下降、就
業指數加速衰退、企業展望減弱，皆拖累 PMI 指數水準。此外，投入、產出物價
指數分別上升至 60.8、54，顯示成本壓力加劇，但售價調整速度依然較為遲緩，
不利於獲利表現。官方非製造業 PMI 上升 1.2 點至 50.2，其中建築業活動指數大
增 3.4 點至 50.3，脫離連續八個月的衰退區間，主要是政府加大財政刺激力度，
推動重大建設開工；非製造業的從業人員指數為 45.9，顯示就業情況仍顯嚴峻，
亦較不利於內需表現。 
鑒於內需動能疲弱，人行於 9 月 29 日宣布下調抵押補充貸款(該貸款本質是
將資金引導至政府希望發展的領域)利率自 1.75%降至 1.50%，並擴大適用範圍，
將水網、新型電網、算力網、新一代通信網、城市地下管網及物流網等建設納入
支持，有助於提振內需動能。未來若內需復甦力道不如預期，不排除人行將進一
步降準或擴大結構性貨幣工具支持經濟；至於全面降息則仍須兼顧人民幣匯率及
中美利差等因素，可能性相對較低。 
五、印度及東南亞—泰國、菲律賓出口均達 2 成以上的高速成長 
(1)新加坡：受服務業、零售暨其他商品業，以及食品價格漲幅擴大影響，8 月消
費者物價指數(CPI)年增率升至 2.3%、不包括住宿及個人交通費用之核心 CPI 年
增率升至 2.2%， 連續3 個月上升並創 2 年新高 。 受全球能源價格居高不下及氣候
條件不佳影響，預期新加坡進口食品、商品及服務成本將持續上升，惟國內生產
率成長及政府補貼政策將抑制部分服務業通膨，新加坡金管局(MAS)或在 10 月
「略微」緊縮貨幣政策。(2)泰國：8 月出口額年增 24.3%(346 億美元)，主要由工
業產品出口成長 27.1%所帶動，其中電腦及相關設備（年增 42.1%）、電話及零
件（151.2%） 及電路板 （19.2%） 增幅最為顯著，對美國出口年增48.7%、對中國
和日本分別成長 15.5%、19.6%。8 月進口額年增 25.1%(371 億美元)，以燃料 （年
增 42.8%） 、 資本貨物 （33.9%） 、 其他貨物 （27.4%） 、 原材料和中間產品 （21.3%）、
運輸設備（13.6%）為主。進出口相抵，8 月貿易逆差 24.8 億美元，較 7 月略收
窄，為連續第 11 個月出現逆差。(3)菲律賓：8 月出口額大增 27.8%(91 億美元)，
創兩年新高，主要得益於電子產品（年增 59.9%） ，尤其是半導體（73.5%）及黃

六、台灣—景氣對策信號連九月呈紅燈 
8 月景氣對策信號續呈紅燈，綜合判斷分數為 41 分，與上月持平，燈號續呈

 삼성전자의 3Q26 영업이익이 103.6조원(+16%qoq)을 기록할 것으로 전
망한다. 메모리 가격 등 전반적인 반도체 업황에 대한 기존 가정은 대체로 
유지했으나, 최근 원화 강세에 따라 원/달러 환율 가정을 1,440원에서 
1,400원으로 낮추고 MX 사업부의 적자 확대를 반영해 실적 추정치를 하
향 조정했다. 또한 2Q26 재고가 3Q26 매출로 인식되는 과정에서 발생하
는 추가 성과급 충당금 부담도 반영했다.  
 DS 부문의 3Q26 영업이익은 104.0조원(+17%qoq)을 기록할 것으로 전
망한다. 서버 DRAM과 eSSD 중심의 가격 인상이 이어지며, DRAM과 
NAND의 Blended ASP는 각각 +17%, +18%QoQ 상승할 것으로 추정한
다. 1cnm 수율 개선과 HBM4의 빠른 램프업도 실적 개선에 기여할 전망이
다. Foundry/LSI는 HBM4 베이스다이 생산 확대, Groq 3 LPU 양산 및 선
단 공정 가격 인상에 힘입어 하반기 매출 성장이 예상된다. 다만 성과급 충
당금과 감가상각비 부담으로 영업적자 축소 폭은 제한적일 것으로 보인다. 
 DX 부문의 3Q26 영업이익은 -1.8조원(적자지속qoq)을 기록할 것으로 추
정한다. 삼성전자는 메모리 가격 급등에도 제품 가격 인상을 제한하며 시장 
점유율 확대에 주력하고 있다. 이러한 가격 정책과 갤럭시 Z 폴드8 등 신제
품 흥행에 힘입어 판매량은 당초 예상치를 상회할 것으로 보인다. 다만 원
가 상승분의 전가가 제한되면서 영업적자 폭은 크게 확대될 전망이다.  
 삼성전자의 4Q26 영업이익은 114.9조원(+11%qoq)을 기록할 것으로 전
망한다. DRAM과 NAND의 Blended ASP는 각각 +11%, +12%QoQ 상승
하며 견조한 흐름을 이어갈 것으로 보인다. 환율 가정 조정에 따라 2026년
과 2027년 영업이익 추정치를 각각 365.2조원(+735%yoy), 595.8조원
(+63%YoY)으로 낮췄다. 4Q26~1Q27 범용 DRAM·NAND 및 2027년 
HBM 가격 협상 환경은 공급업체에 유리하게 전개되고 있어, 향후 가격 가
정의 추가 상향 가능성이 높다고 판단한다.  
 극심한 DRAM 공급 부족으로 인한 HBM 적층 단수 하향 조정(base die 수
요 증가), Memory Wall에 대응하기 위한 3D Stacked DRAM·zHBM 도입 
논의 등은 파운드리의 기술적 가치를 부각시키는 요인이다. 파운드리의 수
익성 회복 속도는 완만할 전망이지만, 메모리와 파운드리를 연계한 턴키 비
즈니스의 시너지는 AI 반도체 시장 내 경쟁력을 높이고, 메모리 시장 점유
율 확대에도 기여할 것으로 보인다. 적극적인 메모리 가격 인상 정책으로 
업계 최고 수준의 Blended ASP도 장기간 유지할 것으로 예상한다. 
 이제 메모리 업종 주가는 업황의 지속 가능성을 확인하고 꾸준한 주주환원
을 반영하며, 중장기적으로 점진적인 상승세를 이어갈 것으로 판단한다. 삼
성전자는 메모리·파운드리를 아우르는 턴키 역량과 가격 정책에 기반한 실
적 차별화를 통해 피어 그룹 내 경쟁력을 높여갈 것으로 보인다.

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

2026.09.11  
HD 현대중공업 (329180)

드디어 엔진 증설, 저평가된 주가  Flash Note 
[조선/기계/방산] 변용진 2122-9181 yongjin.byun@imfnsec.com

Check Point  Buy (Maintain) 
HD현대중공업 중형엔진 및 SMR 공장 증설 결정  
엔진도 끌어올리지 못하는 조선업 주가, 그래도 펀더멘털은 굳건

목표주가(12M) 860,000 원(유지) 
종가(2026.09.10) 453,500 원 
상승여력 89.6 %

HD현대중공업, 중형엔진 및 SMR 시설투자 공시  
기다리던 엔진 증설의 세부 사항이 드디어 공개되었다. HD 현대중공업은 10일 공시를 통해 총 1조 722억원(중형엔진 
8,336억원/SMR 2,386억원) 규모의 시설투자 계획을 밝혔다. 먼저 중형엔진 관련, 글로벌 전력 수요 증가에 따른 엔진발전 시장 
확대에 대응 목적으로 생산능력 확충을 위한 토지 취득 및 공장 신설 및 생산설비 구축에 28년 5월까지 투자가 진행될 예정이다. 
중형엔진의 생산 능력은 현재 연간 3GW급에서 증설이 완료되면 7.2GW급으로 확장되며, 첫 엔진 출하는 28년 하반기로 예상된다. 
증설되는 신공장의 가동률은 28년 30%로 출발하여 30년 100%로 올라설 전망이다. 이에 따라 연간 1GW당 약 1조원의 매출과 
20%이상의 영업이익(현 엔진사업부 이상) 수준으로 추정되며, 계획대로 증설이 완료되고 일감을 확보한다면 총 4조원 이상의 매출 
증대 효과가 기대된다. SMR 투자 관련해서는 SMR 주기기 제작사업 진입 및 수주물량 증가에 대응하기 위해 SMR 전용 공장, 설비 
구축 및 기존 공장 최적화에 29년 4월까지 2,386억원이 투입된다. 이는 연간으로 Terrapower의 Natrium SMR 주기기 2기(각 
345MW)를 제작할 수 있는 규모다. HD현대중공업은 이번 증설이 데이터센터용 엔진만을 위한 것이 아님을 강조했다. 데이터센터 뿐 
아니라 국가전력망, 원전용 비상발전기 등 전세계적으로 증가하는 육상용 발전엔진 수요에 대응하기 위함이며, 최근 미국의 육상 
데이터센터 사업이 님비현상 등으로 일부 좌초되고 있음에도 그보다 더 큰 그림에 베팅한 것이다.  
엔진도 끌어올리지 못하는 조선업 주가, 그래도 펀더멘털은 굳건  
분명한 기업가치 증분의 근거가 되는 증설공시였음에도 불구하고 HD현대중공업의 주가는 발표 이후 하락으로 마감하였다. 올해 
조선업 주가는 YTD 평균 10%이상 하락하며 약세를 보이고 있는 상태다. (HD현대중공업 -10.0%, 삼성중공업 -11.6%, 한화오션 -
25.5%)  
조선사 공히 2분기의 실적 서프라이즈와 함께 올해 수주목표를 사실상 초과 달성하는 등 우수한 펀더멘털을 유지하고 있으나 이는 
주가 상승재료로 반영되고 있지 않다. 7~8월 반도체발 주식시장 혼란에 따른 전체적 수급 약세도 무시할 수 없는 요인이다. 여기에 
지난해 주가 상승의 주요 동력으로 작용하였던 미국 함정 사업 관련 협력 진전이 느린 점 등이 주가 약세의 주요 원인으로 
분석된다. 백악관 및 美해군에서는 지속적으로 함정 해외 건조의 필요성을 역설하고 있으나 美입법부에서는 지역구 의원의 반대 
등으로 관련 법안의 입법 동력이 약해져 있는 상황이다. 美국회의 이번 회기(119대 회기, ~2027년 1월 3일까지)가 이미 막바지에 
다다르고 있으며 11월 중간선거 일정까지 감안하면 실질적으로 이번 회기중에 관련 법안이 통과 및 발효될 가능성은 미미하기 
때문에, 2026년 남은 하반기에 함정사업 관련 조선업 주가 동력을 기대하기는 힘들어졌다. 다가오고 있는 3분기 실적도 원화 강세 
및 여름휴가/사고 등에 따른 조업일수 하락, 후판 유통가 상승 등 감익 요인이 산적해 있어 2분기에 비해 쉽지 않은 상황이다.  
그러나 여전히 본업인 조선업의 체력은 훌륭하다. 전세계 선박 발주량은 8월 누적기준 5,972만 CGT로 전년동기 대비 +60.6% 많은 
발주가 나오고 있으며 이는 2021년 이후 가장 강한 발주세다. 한국 조선사의 주력 선종인 LNG, 탱커, 컨테이너선의 발주 비중이 
전체 발주량의 67%를 차지하고 있으며 이에 힘입어 조선사들은 수주목표를 사실상 초과 달성하였다. (상선기준, HD현대중공업 달성 
130.6억 달러/목표 114.7억, 삼성중공업 달성 61억/목표 57억, 한화오션 달성 60억/목표없음) 3분기 실적은 다소 쉬어갈 수 
있으나 여전히 수주잔고에 기반한 추정상 매출/영업이익은 2028년까지 꾸준한 상승이 예상된다. 작년보다 오히려 하락한 주가가 
현저한 저평가일 수밖에 없는 이유다. 조선업 비중확대 및 HD현대중공업 Top Pick 의견을 유지한다.

 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.

 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

[투자의견] 
종목추천 투자등급  
종목투자의견은 향후 12개월간 추천일 종가대비 해당종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함.  
 Buy(매수): 추천일 종가대비 +15% 이상

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
ECONOMIC INSIGHT
• Any forecast rests on assumptions. Each might be
individually reasonable, but if they all tilt in the same
direction, they look less plausible in combination.
• Different assumptions can also drive different conclusions,
as can be seen in Treasury and RBA views on trend growth

in supply capacity. Treasury’s long-term view on both
productivity growth and labour force participation trends is
systematically less downbeat than the RBA’s (shorter-term)
view. Treasury’s estimate of the stable-inflation rate of
unemployment is also lower than the RBA’s estimate.
• We consider the risks around the RBA’s view to be
asymmetric. The RBA’s assumptions implicitly require a
repeat of the factors that dragged recent productivity
outcomes down, despite strong global investment, policy

changes domestically and the possible benefits of AI. Some
observers appear to extrapolate this weakness beyond the
RBA’s forecast horizon. But this would be hard to square
with the reasonable belief that the global interest rate
structure will be higher in future than it was pre-pandemic.
A systematic tilt, even if unintentional and understandable,
may point to the likely direction of future surprises.
Any forecast rests on assumptions, and the further out

you forecast, the harder it is to ground those assumptions
in something real. Add the uncertainty about AI, where
predictions range from “unbounded prosperity without
needing to work” to “we will all be dead in a couple of years”,
and you can see why assuming the future looks like the past is
an attractive fall-back position.
How much of the past, though? This is the context for the
debate about the Intergenerational Review (IGR)  released this
week. In one corner, Treasury assumes that future productivity

growth will average 1.2%yr over the next 40 years, in line with
the 30-year average, and that labour force participation will
continue to trend up, as it has for the past five decades, until
2040. In the other corner, the RBA assumes that productivity
growth will average 0.7%yr, in line with the past 20 years
– essentially the post-GFC and pandemic period – and that
participation rates will be flat to down in the near term.
Both productivity assumptions have limitations: round-number-

of-year averages might be distorted by capturing a downturn
without the matching upturn, or vice versa. Peak-to-peak or
trough-to-trough is always preferable. Even that calculation
cannot escape the weakness of recent years, though.
When your assumptions have only upside risk
Luci Ellis
Chief Economist, Westpac Group
To be fair, the RBA’s productivity assumptions relate only to
the short term and are not strictly comparable to the IGR,
as the Governor has emphasised this week.  By contrast, the

near-term divergence in forecasts for the participation rate,
which my colleague Ryan Wells  first highlighted in May , is a
genuine divergence.
The RBA’s more downbeat set of assumptions could be
unwittingly contributing to a self-fulfilling talking-down
of Australia’s prospects that clearly appeals to some
commentators outside the public sector. Many in this group
view productivity growth as stemming wholly or mostly from
policy “reforms”, harking back to the halcyon Hawke–Keating

era of deregulation. Proponents point to the lack of reform
recently to justify this downbeat view of growth capacity. We
think this misses the global nature of that slowdown.
Part of the trouble with the downbeat view of future
productivity is that it implicitly assumes that the factors
weighing on productivity growth in the past will continue.
But there are good reasons not to expect the same post-GFC
weakness in risk appetite, and so investment and capital

accumulation, decades later and absent another crisis. The AI
boom and associated resurgence of risk appetite make it hard
to argue that we will see the same lack of new productivity-
enhancing technologies and general malaise as that period.
For Australia specifically, assuming the same low average
productivity growth as the past 20 years would require
assuming a compositional drag s imilar to a repeat of the
run-up of the care economy and decline in measured mining

production over the past 15 years. To be fair, decarbonisation
does imply further declines in (capital-intensive, high-
productivity) coal production, dragging mining productivity
down further. It would be a stretch, though, to assume
expansion in critical and transition minerals provides no offset.
We can grant that AI’s impact on productivity will take a while
to come through and still expect some boost in coming years
– at least by the end of the RBA’s forecast horizon in 2028.

Indeed, some observers already see an AI-induced boost to
service-sector activity in the recent upside surprise in UK GDP.
Some productivity pessimists might want to argue that the
“RBA assumptions collectively imply a
downbeat view of supply growth."

### Page 3

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
3
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
rest of the world will benefit from the AI boom, but somehow
not Australia, or not until later. Such an extraordinary claim
requires a mechanism explaining that divergence, though.
The Productivity Commission estimated back in December
that AI could boost multifactor productivity by 2.3%pt over

the course of the next decade, or roughly a quarter-point per
year extra growth on average – a big difference in the scheme
of usual growth rates in output and productivity. Labour
productivity growth would be boosted a little more; the data
centre boom is expanding the capital stock. But an estimate
published in December is unlikely to adequately capture the
step change in capability of the main models, especially the
coding and agentic harnesses, that emerged over the summer

of 2025/26. Given what we understand to be limited adoption
of AI in the Australian public sector, policymakers might also
be systematically underestimating both the technology’s
capabilities and the breadth of uptake in the private sector.
As an illustration of how downbeat you need to be to
extrapolate the RBA’s near-term assumption further out,
suppose that all AI does is make about 10% of the economy
10% more productive over four years. This extra 1% of

economy-wide productivity is well below most estimates
of the impact. It would nonetheless add a quarter-point on
annual productivity growth for four years, bringing a downbeat
0.7% assumption up to almost 1%.
A deeper challenge is that, beyond the RBA’s near-term
horizon, low productivity growth is hard to square with a
higher future average for interest rates globally.
We note, for example, recent comments by RBA Deputy
Governor Hauser, pointing to 700 years of bond yield data

to suggest that the post-GFC period of low interest rates
was an anomaly, something we noted two years ago. The
global structure of interest rates emerges to balance global
saving and investment, and stronger investment for AI and
other purposes is clearly in evidence. We find it implausible
to assume that all that investment happens, pushing up
global yields, and none of it delivers higher productivity. Even
increased defence spending has historically had productivity

payoffs, as Ukraine’s drone manufacturing ecosystem has
exemplified recently.
It is understandable that when inflation is above target, a
central bank would rather err on the side of caution than
techno-optimism. They know that being downbeat, and
running policy a little too tight, means a faster return of
inflation to target, which is no bad thing for an inflation-
targeting central bank.
It matters, though, when those understandable assumptions

are systematically one-sided. If there are arguments that
productivity growth over the next few years will be even lower
than the 0.7%yr 20-year average, we are yet to see them. In
contrast, there are reasonable arguments for it to be a bit higher.
Similarly, the RBA’s participation rate assumptions are low
given near-term demographic trends , and out of step with
Treasury’s revised projections. Today’s labour market data
for August shows the participation rate rising again, to just

12½ basis points below the January 2025 all-time high. This
supports the idea that RBA’s view on participation trends
is too downbeat. The IGR’s NAIRU (stable-inflation rate of
unemployment) assumption is a quarter-point or so below
RBA estimates revealed in various FOI requests and the
Governor’s comments this week.
Together, the three RBA assumptions imply weaker growth
in supply capacity and thus more inflationary pressure for any
given level of demand. If every one of your assumptions seems

individually plausible, but all skew in the one direction, it
starts to look like a pattern. The thumb on the scale is unlikely
to be intentional, but it does suggest the likely direction of
future surprises in the medium term.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
THE WEEK THAT WAS
Cliff Notes: the importance of supply
This week, market participants have focused on discussions
between the US and China on trade, security and technology
as President Xi visits Washington.
Preliminary discussions between US Treasury Secretary

Bessent and Chinese Vice Premier He Lifeng ahead of
President Xi’s arrival in Washington were characterised by
both sides as productive, and Treasury Secretary Bessent
subsequently announced that discussions in Washington
would leave trade negotiations for upcoming meetings
between the two leaders in Shenzhen and Miami. To allow
room for this deferral, the current trade truce will be extended
until January 2027. President Trump subsequently outlined

that discussions during President Xi’s Washington visit would
instead focus on security, technology and AI.
A more constructive tone was also seen between the US and
Iran overnight, if press reports are accurate. According to
Bloomberg and Reuters, sources suggest the US and Iran are
again exploring a staged deal which would see Iran re-open
the Strait of Hormuz and the US lift its blockade of Iranian
ports. Negotiations are occurring on the sidelines of the UN

General Assembly, with Qatari officials mediating.
President Trump continues to show little interest in a deal
before November’s mid-term elections, but the global
economy is under increasing pressure as the price of Brent oil
hovers between USD100 and USD110 while refining spreads
add additional impetus to refined product prices. The mooted
consideration of a potential US diesel export ban this week
makes clear that the US is not immune from this pressure

despite having significant domestic energy supply and refining
capacity, relative to their own demand.
The consequences of the current energy supply shock and other
disruptions the US have experienced over the past year remain
front of mind for FOMC members. Those speaking this week such
as NY and Cleveland Fed Presidents Williams and Hammack
highlighted the risk to inflation expectations from a series of
supply shocks when the economy is fully employed, and the

likelihood of an additional hike(s) being required to rein in current
inflation momentum and risks. Williams noted that expectations
of another hike by year end are "reasonable" and that the FOMC
have "a lot of work to do". Debt market’s concern remains acute,
having now almost priced in four hikes over the coming year and
taken the US 10-year above 5.00%, currently 5.20%.
Australia’s only data release this week was the August
Labour Force Survey . Employment surprised to the upside,

rising 39.5k. But, once again, new labour demand was more
than offset by supply as the participation rate bounced
0.2ppts higher to 67.1%, just shy of January 2025’s 115-year
high of 67.2%. As a result, the unemployment rate increased
from 4.5% to 4.6% and was close to rounding up to 4.7%
(4.65% to two decimal places).
There may be some residual seasonality in August’s print
related to the new survey collection model. However, the
underlying trend is still consistent with a gradual easing in the

labour market, seeing the unemployment rate drift higher. We
expect this to continue through to the first half of 2027.
The strength in labour force participation also speaks to the
broader issue of Australia’s supply capacity. As discussed
by Chief Economist Luci Ellis , the RBA’s assumptions around
participation, productivity and the NAIRU collectively
skew towards a weak assessment of supply capacity. For
participation in particular, the RBA’s near-term assumptions

are downbeat compared to recent demographic trends and
also sit below Treasury’s longer-term projections as set out in
the 2026 IGR. As above, the August LFS emphasises that slack
could emerge more quickly than the RBA’s forecasts imply
without an outright fall in employment.
Ahead of next week’s RBA meeting and inflation data,
our latest state-level analysis  provides perspective on the
persistence of underlying price pressures, particularly the

importance of housing-related components. For a broader
assessment of the fiscal backdrop and the implications for the
states, our latest state fiscal update  explores how the housing
market correction and changes to NDIS funding arrangements
are pressuring state finances.
Before turning to this week’s events offshore, it is important
to highlight that last Friday the Bank of Japan raised its
policy rate by 25bp to 1.25%. Policymakers maintained a

constructive assessment of the economy, pointing to resilient
consumption, ongoing labour market tightness, and solid
investment spending. More importantly, the Bank expressed
growing confidence that inflation is increasingly being
sustained by domestic wage and demand dynamics rather
than imported cost shocks alone.
In our view, the emphasis placed on improving underlying
inflation dynamics suggests policymakers are becoming
increasingly comfortable that inflation is able to be sustained by

domestic demand and wage growth. Normalisation is likely to
remain gradual, but the Bank appears firmly on a path towards
further tightening, with rates likely to reach 1.5% by mid-2027.
Elliot Clarke, Head of International Economics
Ryan Wells, Economist
Illiana Jain, Economist

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
FOCUS ON NEW ZEALAND
Along for the ride
There was plenty of action in the markets this week, as global
interest rates continued to move higher, and New Zealand
was taken along for the ride. In the US, strong economic data
and resurgent oil prices have led the market to believe that

the central bank will need to get on with the job and hike
its policy rate further in the months ahead. Longer-term US
interest rates have continued to lift sharply, driving both a rise
in domestic interest rates and a fall in the New Zealand dollar,
with the trade-weighted index already 4% below what the
RBNZ assumed in its September Monetary Policy Statement.
While the September MPS worked to dampen market
expectations about the pace of future rate hikes, offshore

developments have seen market pricing for the OCR bounce back
and exceed previous levels. Interest rate markets are currently
pricing around a 75% chance of a hike at the 28 October policy
review, with a high chance of another hike on top of this at the 9
December review. We’d question whether the RBNZ’s thinking
has really shifted to that degree in the last few weeks though.
Notably, the RBNZ provided some unscheduled comments on
the monetary policy outlook this week, in what was otherwise

a very light local news week. In connection with a visit to
Dunedin by Governor Breman and other RBNZ leaders, the
RBNZ put out a media release that included the following:
“If higher oil prices persist, they are expected to result in
somewhat higher near-term inflation than we assumed in the
September Statement… As always, we will assess incoming
data and global developments ahead of our next decision in
October and remain focused on the outlook for inflation over

the medium term.”
Both of these statements are fairly benign and factual in
themselves. What’s more notable is that the RBNZ felt the
need to make a public comment at all; the occasion didn’t
require one. (The Governor’s presentation at a business lunch
was originally billed as being based on material from the
September MPS, with no new insights.)
If the intention was to guide market expectations for the
October meeting lower, it wasn’t entirely successful. Indeed,

the market initially fixated on the first part of the statement –
higher near-term inflation – and pushed pricing for an October
hike even higher on the day. While this was unwound the
following day, market pricing for OCR hikes remains higher
than it was at the start of the week.
As it stands, that leaves the RBNZ with the prospect of
rattling the market if it follows through with the October
pause that it signalled in September MPS. However, it’s

possible that the local and offshore economic data, together
with geopolitical developments, could shift market pricing in
the meantime. Domestically, the NZIER’s quarterly survey of
business opinion (6 Oct) and the September quarter CPI (22
Oct) will be especially important in that regard.
Turning to the data, the only release this week was our
Employment Confidence Index, produced in conjunction with
McDermott Miller. Compared to our consumer confidence

survey, there was a relatively weak bounce in employment
confidence in the September quarter. The index rose by 3.2
points to 86.3, leaving it at the second-lowest level on record
for this survey going back to 2004.
Perceptions of current job opportunities were unchanged at a
net -60%. This is the component of the survey that seems to
have the most predictive power for near-term labour market
conditions and is broadly consistent with the unemployment

rate holding at its current 11-year high of 5.6%. Hiring has
remained soft to an unusual degree for this stage of the
economic cycle, and those who are out of the workforce have
been finding it increasingly difficult to get in.
It’s clear that there is still substantial slack in the labour
market and little upward pressure on wages, which would
push back somewhat against the market’s view that the RBNZ
will need to pick up the pace and deliver two more OCR hikes

before Christmas. The RBNZ doesn’t need to have employment
formally in its mandate for it to be attentive to the signals
coming from the labour market.
The data flow picks up again next week, with monthly releases
for employment, building activity, and business and consumer
confidence – see our previews on the following page. The
other release of note is the Pre-Election Fiscal and Economic
Update (PREFU) on Tuesday. This ‘book opening’ exercise
is designed to create a level playing field, allowing parties

outside the current Government and the public more generally
to judge to the state of the public finances in the leadup to the
General Election on 7 November. In the case of the opposition
parties, it will also allow them to calibrate the spending and
taxation policies on which they will campaign. See our preview
for the details on what we expect from this release.
Michael Gordon
Senior Economist

AUS: Aug Household Spending Indicator (%mth)
Sep 29, Last: 1.1%, WBC f/c: –0.5%
Mkt f/c: 0.3%, Range: –0.5% to 0.7%
The household spending indicator rose 1.1%mth in July,
comfortably exceeding expectations and extending the strong
momentum evident through the middle of the year. Annual

growth accelerated to 7.0%yr, the fastest pace since early
2023.
Our Westpac-DataX Card Tracker points to softer spending
conditions through August and early September, with higher
fuel prices increasingly driving spending growth and, at the
margin, squeezing spending elsewhere. The detail suggests
a modest rotation away from discretionary categories, with
hospitality spending easing and vehicle sales also likely
moderating after a strong June quarter. Together with a softer

read from our MHSI-aligned card measure, we anticipate the
household spending indicator will decline 0.5%mth in August.
AUS: RBA Policy Decision (%)
Sep 29, Last: 4.35, Westpac f/c: 4.60
Market f/c: 4.60, Range: 4.60
Westpac anticipates the RBA Monetary Policy Board will raise
the cash rate by 25bps to 4.60% at its September meeting.
As discussed by Chief Economist Luci Ellis recent rhetoric from
the RBA have escalated, particularly with Governor Bullock

flagging that upside inflation risks appear to be "materialising"
– the conditionality set in August for further rate hikes.
There is a risk of a follow-up hike, though it crucially depends
on the data flow after the meeting. For September, we
continue to expect a split vote at the meeting, with material
differences in opinion about trend growth in supply capacity,
as discussed on Page 2.
AUS: Aug Headline CPI (%mth)
Sep 30, Last: 1.0, Westpac f/c: 0.4

Market f/c: 0.5, Range: 0.4 to 0.9
The July CPI reversed May and June weakness with a larger
than expected 1.0% increase. This was stronger than
Westpac’s expectation of 0.8% and market’s expectation of
0.9%. The annual pace eased to 3.5%yr from 3.8%yr.
We are looking for a 0.4% increase in the August CPI. Food &
non-alcoholic beverages to rise 0.2%mth with solid gains for
bread & cereal products (0.4%), meat & seafood (0.5%) and
food products n.e.c. (0.4%) partially offset by falling non-

alcoholic beverages (–0.6%) and a soft print for meals out &
take away food (0.1%).
At two decimal points it is a 0.43% increase in August so the
risk this month is to the upside.
For more details please see “ August CPI Preview ”.

AUS: Aug Trimmed Mean CPI (%mth)
Sep 30, Last: 0.5, Westpac f/c: 0.2
Market f/c: 0.3, Range: 0.2 to 0.4
The Trimmed Mean (TM) lifted 0.5%mth/3.5%yr, on par with
last July which was the strongest monthly read in the series’
short history. The result was stronger than both our and the

market’s expectation of 0.4%mth.
August TM is expected to rise 0.2%mth lifting the annual pace
to 3.6%yr. For market services excluding volatiles and holiday
travel we have pencilled in a step down to 0.1%mth, this series
lifted 0.8% in July possibly due to the larger-than-expected
increase in minimum wages.
The distribution of the August TM is from a low of –0.32%mth
to a high of 0.53%mth. Remembering the TM is seasonally
adjusted, with rents (+0.17%mth) and dwellings (+0.20%mth)

mostly locking in the TM. See “ August CPI Preview ”.
AUS: Aug Dwelling Approvals (%mth)
Sep 30, Last: –3.6, Westpac f/c: –5.0
Market f/c: –1.0, Range: –6.0 to 1.0
Dwelling approvals fell 3.6%mth in July, with annual growth
easing to 9.0%yr. The decline was driven primarily by detached
dwellings, although unit approvals also edged lower. The
result suggests higher interest rates may be starting to weigh
on new housing demand.
We expect a similar story to play out in August, with

elevated interest rates continuing to weigh on detached
dwelling approvals, while the unit segment holds broadly
steady. Overall, dwelling approvals are expected to decline
5%mth. While risks remain tilted to the downside, residential
construction should remain relatively resilient through 2026,
supported by the strong lift in approvals through the first half
of the year and a sizeable pipeline of work yet to be done.
AUS: Aug Private Sector Credit (%mth)

Sep 30, Last: 0.6, Westpac f/c: 0.5
Market f/c: 0.5, Range: 0.3 to 0.6
Growth in private sector credit growth appeared unsustainable
over May-Jun, especially given the slowing in the housing
market. Come July, the pace now dropped from 0.8%mth to
0.6%mth. Housing credit the main source of weakness, with
investor credit growth beginning to adjust lower in response to
higher interest rates, lower house prices and, most importantly,
tax changes for investment properties. Meanwhile, business

borrowing continued to rise rapidly.
For August, we have pencilled in a 0.5%mth increase, and we
look for continued divergence between housing and business
segments.

AUS: Sep Cotality Home Value Index (%mth)

The Cotality home value index – covering all of Australia – fell

The September update is set to see more of the same with
the daily measure (covering the five major capital cities rather
than all of Australia) tracking a 1.2% decline in line with
this measure’s August move. Annual growth looks like to
stall about flat. The detail suggests monthly declines have
accelerated in Brisbane, Perth and Adelaide but moderated a
touch in Melbourne, noting that there is a seasonal element

to price moves in Sep. Markets are still adjusting to higher
interest rates and the tax policy changes introduced in the May
Federal budget with more weakness likely into year-end.
AUS: Aug Goods Trade Balance ($bn)
Oct 1, Last: 1.9, Westpac f/c: 2.3
Market f/c: 2.2, Range: 1.5 to 2.5
The goods trade surplus narrowed slightly to $1.9bn in July
but remained above our and consensus expectations. This
was down from an upwardly revised surplus of $2.3bn in June.

Underneath the modest headline change was considerable
volatility among components, especially in flows of non-
monetary gold, remain the chief culprit.
For August, we have pencilled in a slight widening of the trade
surplus to $2.3bn. Port data points to a bounce-back in coal
and iron ore export volumes,  and prices looked to have edged
higher. However, volatility in non-monetary gold trade and
other hard-to-predict sub-categories (like ADP equipment)

remain a wildcard.
NZ: Aug Monthly Employment Indicator (%mth)
Sep 28, Last: 0.3, Westpac f/c: 0.3
The MEI has continued to tick higher in recent months,
showing that the jobs market has held its ground through
the Iran conflict and the consequent surge in fuel prices. The
weekly snapshots provided by Stats NZ have been running
ahead of year-ago levels and suggest another solid result
for August – we’ve pencilled in a 0.3% increase, following
a similar rise in July. However, the history of this indicator

suggests that both months are likely to be revised down a bit
from their initial release.

### Page 9

Nonfarm payrolls surprised to the upside in August, 
registering a gain of 162k with 55k in back revisions to June 
and July. The report left the 3-month average at 71k, near the 
top of the estimated range consitent with balance between 
labour demand and supply. Come September, the trend is 
expected to remain in place with a 80k gain forecast. With 
inflation risks paramount, upward revisions will have a greater 
impact on market variables than a negative revision.
The decline in the participation rate over the past year has 
capped the unemployment rate. Participation is likely to edge 
higher in September and take the unemployment rate up 
to 4.2% with it. There is little available slack in the labour 
market, but plenty just outside if demand lifts.

Document 201: CTCP Nhựa Bình Minh - Báo cáo cập nhật 2Q2026 (2026-09-23)

Source PDF: all report pdf/CTCP Nhựa Bình Minh - Báo cáo cập nhật 2Q2026 (2026-09-23).pdf
Report source: KBSV (Company)
Date: 2026-09-23
Pages: 9
Status: ok

### Page 1

Doanh thu 2Q2026 tăng trưởng chậm 
nhưng biên lợi nhuận gộp vẫn được 
duy trì do tăng giá bán

Sản lượng tiêu thụ 6M2026 chững lại 
do tâm lý nhập hàng thận trọng và 
hoạt động xây dựng phục hồi chậm

Biên lợi nhuận gộp được dự báo sẽ 
thu hẹp cuối năm, phản ánh chi phí 
nguyên liệu cao hơn trong 1Q2026

Khuyến nghị TRUNG LẬP, giá mục 
tiêu 166,100 VND/cổ phiếu 
 Doanh thu/LNST 2Q2026 đạt lần lượt 1,320/367 tỷ VND (+1%/+11% YoY). Lũy kế 
6M2026, doanh thu đạt 2,777 tỷ VND (+3% YoY) trong khi LNST ước đạt 671 tỷ VND 
(+9% YoY). Biên lãi gộp 6M2026 cải thiện đạt 47.0% (+240 bps YoY), chủ yếu do việc 
tăng giá bán giúp doanh nghiệp bù đắp được mức tăng của chi phí đầu vào.

Lũy kế 6M2026, sản lượng tiêu thụ của BMP ước đạt 43,920 tấn, giảm 5% svck. Việc 
BMP thu hẹp các chương trình chiết khấu, với mức chiết khấu bình quân khoảng 
5.2% trong 2Q2026, làm giảm động lực nhập hàng của các đại lý và khiến sản lượng 
tiêu thụ chậm lại. Dù vậy, thị phần toàn quốc của BMP ước đạt 28% (+1 đpt svck) 
nhờ các doanh nghiệp khác trong ngành gặp khó khăn khi giá hạt nhựa tăng mạnh.

Biên lợi nhuận gộp của BMP dự kiến thu hẹp trong 2H2026 trước áp lực chi phí 
nguyên liệu đầu vào tăng. Tuy nhiên, chúng tôi kỳ vọng mức độ ảnh hưởng sẽ thấp 
hơn so với các doanh nghiệp trong ngành do (1) các lô nguyên liệu giá cao từ 
1Q2026 đã được đưa vào sản xuất và tiêu thụ một phần trong 1H2026; (2) BMP chủ 
động nâng gấp đôi hàng tồn kho để ứng phó với biến động bất lợi của giá nguyên 
liệu; (3) hơn 90% nhu cầu nguyên liệu uPVC được cung ứng từ các đối tác trong 
nước như hệ sinh thái SCG tại Việt Nam, AGC Chemicals và LSP; và (4) công ty duy 
trì giá bán bình quân ở mức cao.

Dựa trên kết quả kinh doanh và định giá, chúng tôi khuyến nghị TRUNG LẬP với cổ 
phiếu BMP, giá mục tiêu là 166,100 VND/cổ phiếu (tương ứng với mức tăng 14.3% 
so với giá đóng cửa ngày 23/09/2026).

Tăng trưởng doanh thu và lợi nhuận thuần 5 năm gần nhất Hoạt động kinh doanh

Nhựa Bình Minh (BMP) là doanh nghiệp hàng đầu 
trong lĩnh vực sản xuất ống nhựa với thị phần 
dẫn đầu tại thị trường nội địa (27% vào cuối 
2023), thị trường chính tập trung tại khu vực 
miền Nam. BMP có 4 nhà máy với tổng công suất 
đạt 150,000 tấn/năm. 
Nguồn: BMP, KBSV

Điểm nhấn đầu tư  Chú thích 
Kỳ vọng đà phục hồi của xây dựng dân dụng và bất động sản miền Nam sẽ thúc đẩy sản lượng tiêu 
thụ tăng trưởng trong 2026–2027. 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo 
Giá PVC được kỳ vọng sẽ hạ nhiệt và khả năng giữ giá bán đầu ra ở mức cao giúp BMP giữ biên lợi 
nhuận gộp ở nền cao. 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo

### Page 4

4

Nhu cầu nhập hàng thận trọng và 
cắt giảm các đợt chiết khấu trong 
kỳ của BMP làm giảm sản lượng tiêu 
thụ trong 2Q2026

Sự hồi phục của nhu cầu xây dựng 
dân dụng và nguồn cung các dự án 
bất động sản tại miền Nam sẽ thúc 
đẩy tăng trưởng sản lượng 2H2026 
 Sản lượng tiêu thụ trong 2Q2026 ước đạt 20,100 tấn, giảm 11% svck, nâng tổng 
sản lượng tiêu thụ 6M2026 ước đạt 43,920 tấn, giảm 5% svck do các yếu tố bất 
lợi từ bên ngoài như (1) giá hạt nhựa tăng cao khiến thị trường dè chừng trong 
việc nhập hàng vì lo ngại nguồn cung bất ổn (2) BMP đã thu hẹp các chương 
trình chiết khấu so với 2025 ngay trước thời điểm xung đột bùng nổ, giúp tỷ lệ 
chi phí bán hàng/doanh thu về mức 11.3%, giảm 800 bps so với 1Q2026 ở mức 
19.3%, nhưng đồng thời cũng khiến sản lượng tiêu thụ của BMP suy giảm do 
chính sách bán hàng thắt chặt hơn với các đối thủ có thị phần thấp hơn.

Chúng tôi kỳ vọng sản lượng tiêu thụ của BMP dần hồi phục từ 3Q2026 khi nhu 
cầu nhập hàng của các đại lý cải thiện sau giai đoạn thận trọng trong nửa đầu 
năm. Về cuối năm, hoạt động xây dựng dự kiến tăng tốc khi điều kiện thi công 
thuận lợi hơn sau mùa mưa. Bên cạnh đó, việc triển khai các dự án nhà ở tại 
TP.HCM và các tỉnh lân cận được kỳ vọng hỗ trợ nhu cầu ống nhựa trong giai 
đoạn tới. Theo đó, chúng tôi dự phóng sản lượng tiêu thụ của BMP lần lượt đạt 
96,695/100,125/96,750 tấn trong 2026/2027/2028.

Biểu đồ 3. Tương quan giữa tỷ lệ chi phí bán hàng/doanh 
thu và lợi nhuận thuần/sản lượng tiêu thụ  
 Biểu đồ 4. Dự phóng nguồn cung bất động sản tại Hà Nội và Hồ 
Chí Minh

Nguồn: BMP, KBSV  Nguồn: CBRE, KBSV

   BMP duy trì vòng quay dẫn đầu trong 
ngành nhờ lợi thế nguồn nguyên liệu và thị 
phần đứng đầu tại miền Nam. Tuy nhiên, 
xu hướng suy giảm các quý gần đây về 
mức 5.3 lần, giảm 0.21 điểm QoQ do nhu 
cầu tiêu thụ toàn ngành suy giảm.

### Page 5

5

Lợi thế từ hệ sinh thái công ty mẹ 
SCG và đối tác Nhật Bản giúp BMP 
có nhiều dư địa để nâng cao hiệu 
quả hoạt động

Theo chia sẻ từ ban lãnh đạo, BMP có lợi thế vì nhập nguyên liệu từ công ty mẹ 
là hệ sinh thái SCG tại Việt Nam và đối tác Nhật Bản là AGC Chemicals Vietnam, 
giúp giảm được 5% thuế nhập khẩu và chi phí vận chuyển. Điều này giúp BMP 
hưởng lợi kép khi có thể tăng được giá bán theo thị trường nhưng nguyên vật 
liệu rẻ hơn so với các doanh nghiệp trong ngành. KBSV nhận thấy định hướng 
dài hạn của BMP là cân bằng lợi ích cổ đông và khách hàng, vì vậy việc giảm giá 
bán để giành thị phần không phải lựa chọn ưu tiên của doanh nghiệp mà là 
nâng cao tỷ suất lợi nhuận/sản phẩm thành phẩm.

KBSV dự báo biên lợi nhuận gộp thu hẹp trong 2H2026 khi chi phí nguyên liệu 
giá cao từ 1H2026 được phản ánh vào giá vốn, và hồi phục trong 2027–2028 
nhờ đặc tính giữ giá bán ít thay đổi của nhóm nhựa. Chúng tôi kỳ vọng giá PVC 
resin chịu áp lực giảm từ (1) nhu cầu xây dựng tại Trung Quốc còn yếu khi thị 
trường bất động sản chưa hồi phục rõ ràng; và (2) các nhà sản xuất giải phóng 
hàng tồn kho sau mùa mưa, làm tăng sản lượng hạt nhựa PVC resin. Theo đó, 
KBSV dự phóng biên lợi nhuận gộp của BMP lên 46.4%/46.7%/46.5% trong 
2026/2027/2028.

gộp của BMP

Document 209: ECONOMIC WEEK AHEAD_ October 5-9

Source PDF: all report pdf/ECONOMIC WEEK AHEAD_ October 5-9.pdf
Report source: Substack Reports
Date: 2026-10-04
Pages: 8
Status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: ECONOMIC WEEK AHEAD: October 5-9
Date:    13 hours ago
ECONOMIC WEEK AHEAD: October 5-9

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
ECONOMIC WEEK AHEAD:
October 5-9
By Ed Yardeni & Toby Hearst • 4 Oct 2026
View in browser

### Page 2

Photo by Monica Sauro / Unsplash

### Page 3

Most of last week's economic indicator reports were strong: Initial jobless
claims fell to near a 57-year low, ADP private payrolls rose 90,000 in
September, and the ISM M-PMI was in expansion for a ninth straight
month. Friday's employment report was the exception. Nonfarm payrolls
rose just 29,000 in September, well below expectations, while July and
August were revised down by a combined 60,000. The unemployment
rate edged up to 4.2%.
Odds of a 25bps hike in the federal funds rate (FFR) at the October 27-28

FOMC meeting fell to 22%, with odds of one at the December meeting at
67%. Odds of two hikes by December is at just 3%. In other words, the
market still expects a rate hike this year, though it has all but ruled out
two. However, we still think the Fed ought to hike at least enough to
reverse last year's three 25bps "insurance" cuts. September's hike
reversed one, which leaves two more to go.
This week's data calendar is light, with Wednesday's FOMC minutes the

main event. Here's more:
(1) FOMC minutes. September's FOMC meeting minutes (Wed) should
reveal how much support the Committee has for tightening beyond what
the dot plot in the September meeting’s Summary of Economic
Projections implies.
The median projection of meeting participants showed the FFR at 4.1% at
the end of this year and next, i.e., one more 25bps hike in 2026 followed
by a hold lasting throughout 2027 (chart). Fed Chair Kevin Warsh said the

September hike "removed a dose of accommodation," implying that the
FFR remains below the neutral rate. The median longer-run neutral rate
estimate in September’s dot plot rose to 3.25%, a post-pandemic high.

### Page 4

The FFR futures market’s prediction is more hawkish than the Fed
officials’ median. FFR futures imply 3.3 rate hikes over the next 12 months
and 1.7 over the next six months (chart).
The 2-year Treasury yield is at 4.78%, well above the 4.00% top of the
FFR target range, with 12-month futures at 4.70% (chart).

### Page 5

(2) PMIs. S&P Global's flash NM-PMI jumped to 58.7 in September from
56.5 in August, suggesting that ISM's NM-PMI (Mon), which was 55.4 in
August, remained strong last month (chart). Services prices-paid
components deserve close attention, as the supercore PCED inflation rate
has been rising since October 2025.
(3) ADP employment & jobless claims. ADP's weekly employment
reading (Tue), covering the weeks ended September 12 and 19, follows a
rise in the four-week average to 20,000 for the week ended September 5,

up from a late-July bottom of 8,250 (chart). That pace closely matched
September's ADP private payrolls gain of 90,000.

### Page 6

Initial jobless claims (Thu) follow last week's 197,000, the lowest since
July 18. The four-week average fell to 200,000. Continuing claims
dropped to 1,701,000 for the week ended September 18, the lowest since
March 2023, with the four-week average at 1,739,000. The low level of
initial claims confirmed September's low unemployment rate of 4.2%
(chart).
(4) Global. Eurozone retail sales ex autos fell 0.6% m/m in July, following
June's record high (chart). August's data (Tue) should show whether that

was just a blip.

### Page 7

Bank of England Governor Andrew Bailey may signal whether rising
energy prices warrant a hike when he speaks at the Central Bank of
Turkey's conference (Thu), after three Monetary Policy Committee
members voted for one in September. The markets put the odds of a
November hike at 88%, with roughly 66bps of additional tightening priced
in for 2027.
Canada's employment report for September (Fri) follows August's 41,700
job loss, with the unemployment rate holding at 6.4% (chart).

Document 239: GLP-1之后的下一张牌：礼来、诺和诺德为何同时押注胰淀素

Source PDF: all report pdf/GLP-1之后的下一张牌：礼来、诺和诺德为何同时押注胰淀素.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-03
Pages: 2
Status: ok

### Page 1

下⼀代肥胖药物的⽬标不是取代 GLP-1 ，⽽是叠加新的减重机制。
摘要：
礼来本周公布的⼆期临床数据为 GLP-1 之后的肥胖药物路线图添上了关键⼀笔：其实验性胰淀素
药物 eloralintide 与替尔泊肽联合⽤药，最⾼剂量组 48 周平均减重 23.3% ，远超替尔泊肽单药⾼剂
量组的 14.8% 。
下⼀代肥胖药物的⽬标不是取代 GLP-1 ，⽽是叠加新的减重机制。
礼来和诺和诺德正围绕胰淀素 —— ⼀种与胰岛素⼀同在胰腺中释放、帮助调节饥饿感和饱腹感
的激素 —— 开发⼀系列注射剂、⼝服药和组合⽅案，为减重效果设置更⾼的天花板，同时为对
GLP-1 反应不佳的患者提供替代⽅案。
礼来⼆期数据：减重 23.3% ，但耐受性待解
礼来正双线推进 eloralintide—— 既作为独⽴疗法，也与旗下重磅药物替尔泊肽联合使⽤。本周公
布的⼆期临床试验针对肥胖合并 2 型糖尿病患者，最⾼剂量组合组 48 周平均减重 23.3% ，替尔泊
肽单药⾼剂量组为 14.8% 。上述数据基于疗效分析，即假设所有⼊组患者都完成了全程治疗。

杨百翰⼤学代谢健康专家 Benjamin Bikman 评价称， " 这些结果令⼈⿎舞。 2 型糖尿病患者在这类
疗法上的减重幅度通常低于⾮糖尿病患者。 "
Leerink Partners 分析师 David Risinger 预测，礼来的 eloralintide 系列产品到 2035 年底年销售额
将达 232 亿美元，单药预计 2029 年上市，组合疗法 2030 年跟进。他认为独⽴ eloralintide 的潜⼒
可能更⼤，因为 " 可能有超过 1000 万⼈尝试过 GLP-1 后因疗效不⾜、耐受性问题或基因因素⽽未
能获得成功 " 。
礼来⼼脏代谢健康总裁 Ken Custer 在采访中表⽰： " 患者可能⽆法从替尔泊肽中获得所需的全部
疗效，也可能⽆法从 eloralintide 单药中获得。 "Bikman 也认为，组合疗法为替尔泊肽单药减重进
⼊平台期的患者提供了新的可能。
但礼来需要证明的还很多。数据来⾃规模较⼩的⼆期试验，三期试验将于今年晚些时候启动。
耐受性是关键挑战：因副作⽤停药的⽐例在不同剂量的联合⽤药组为 10.8% ⾄ 27% ，⽽替尔泊肽

单药组仅为 2.9% 。布莱根妇⼥医院体重管理中⼼联合主任 Caroline Apovian 博⼠直⾔， "27% 不
是⼀个好数字。 "Bikman 也强调， " ⼀种疗法只有在患者能够持续使⽤时才是有效的，因此三期
试验中的耐受性将与疗效同等重要。 "
诺和诺德的胰淀素布局： CagriSema 明年上市
诺和诺德在胰淀素领域的布局已持续多年。其胰淀素药物 cagrilintide 在⼀项晚期临床试验中作为
独⽴疗法已显⽰出显著减重效果。将 cagrilintide 与司美格鲁肽（ semaglutide ）联合使⽤的
CagriSema ，在临床研究中展现了更⼤的减重幅度，预计明年初上市，独⽴ cagrilintide 和⾼剂量
CagriSema 预计 2028 年推出。
诺和诺德本周发布的新数据显⽰， CagriSema 的作⽤不仅限于体重下降。在⼀项为期⼀年的脑
部成像（ fMRI ）研究中，该药物减少了 " ⻝物噪⾳ "—— 对⻝物的持续性想法 —— 并改善了器官和
⻣骼健康。研究表明， CagriSema 改变了肥胖或超重⼈群⼤脑中与渴望、愉悦和⾃我控制相关

⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⽣活家 搜索

### Page 2

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
22 收藏 分享到 :
写评论
图⽚
区域对⾼热量⻝物的反应。 " ⼤脑中的信号以⼀种与⽣活质量改善相关联的⽅式发⽣了变化， " 诺
和诺德⾸席科学官 Martin Holst Lange 在采访中说。
诺和诺德还在开发 amycretin （⼜称 zenagamtide ），⼀种⽤单个分⼦同时模拟 GLP-1 和胰淀素两
种激素效果的药物，以每周⼀次注射和每⽇⼝服⽚剂两种形式进⾏测试，今年早些时候公布了
积极的⼆期临床结果。
多靶点时代：从双靶到三靶
胰淀素并⾮新事物。美国在⼆⼗多年前就批准了⾸个胰淀素疗法，但该药物需要每⽇多次注
射，普及度受限。⽬前在研的新⼀代胰淀素药物均为⻓效制剂，可每周注射⼀次。
胰淀素帮助产⽣饱腹感、抑制⻝欲、减缓胃排空，这些效果与 GLP-1 类似，但两者在体内的作⽤

机制完全不同。 " 结果相同，但路径不同， "Bikman 对 CNBC 表⽰。将两种机制叠加的逻辑很直
观：同时调动多种激素信号可能带来更⼤的减重效果，同时避免把单⼀药物的剂量推到极限。
这⼀思路已延伸⾄胰淀素之外。替尔泊肽本⾝已是同时针对 GLP-1 和 GIP 两种激素的药物，礼来
的实验性药物 retatrutide 更进⼀步，同时针对 GLP-1 、 GIP 和胰⾼⾎糖素（ glucagon ）三种激
素，在临床试验中报告了迄今最⼤幅度的减重数据，据已发表数据，该药物在减轻肝脏脂肪、
⽢油三酯和空腹胰岛素⽅⾯表现突出。辉瑞、阿斯利康和 Viking Therapeutics 也在开发各⾃的胰
淀素产品。
⽬前判断胰淀素组合药物是否优于替尔泊肽或其他下⼀代疗法还为时尚早，更多临床试验和监
管审批仍在前⽅。但从双靶到三靶，从注射到⼝服，肥胖药物的竞争格局正在快速分化。

### Page 4

◆ The economy remains on the recovery track despite weakness in some
areas
Annualized real GDP growth in the second quarter was +1.1% QoQ, with growth
staying in positive territory for three straight quarters. Although personal
consumption exhibited weakness and capital investment declined, overall growth
was lifted by foreign demand. Exports, particularly of services, increased, and the
drop in imports of Middle Eastern crude oil also helped foreign demand turn positive.

◆ Housing starts are weak
Housing starts surged 18.6% YoY in June. They have continued to rise on a YoY
basis, but the jump was mainly due to a rebound from the slump in the same month
of the previous year. The recovery in the number of starts is actually moderate,
reflecting higher interest rates and soaring construction costs.
◆ Earthquake impact will need to be watched
Damage from the earthquake that occurred in Kumamoto Prefecture at the end of

July is weighing on economic activity in the affected areas. Although moves toward
recovery can be seen, with many companies having already resumed operations,
the impact could become prolonged in the following scenarios:
The first is if damage to capital stock such as factories and roads has been severe.
Some companies have sustained damage to production facilities, making an early
resumption of operations difficult in some cases.
The second is if a downturn in tourism demand occurs. According to some media

reports, there has been an uptick in cancellations of accommodation reservations in
Kumamoto Prefecture and neighboring prefectures since the earthquake. After the
2016 Kumamoto earthquake, the decline in travel demand from Japanese visitors
was short lived, but lower demand from foreign tourists persisted for a long period.
Annualized real GDP growth was +1.1% quarter over quarter (QoQ) in Q2 2026

### Page 5

Monthly Report of Prospects for Japan's Economy August 2026
The Japan Research Institute, Limited
Exports and inbound demand remain solid despite weakness in some areas
◆ Goods exports are on the verge of picking up
Goods exports are treading water. A sharp rise in memory chip prices is among
factors pushing up export prices, resulting in record high levels of exports in value
terms recently. On the other hand, the increase in volume terms is limited. One

possible reason is that Japan's export competitiveness has declined in fields such as
semiconductors, where global demand is growing.
Looking ahead, exports are expected to pick up. Capital investment by chipmakers
is expanding worldwide, and this should lift exports of semiconductor manufacturing
equipment, an area in which Japan has strength. However, continued caution is
needed regarding the effects of prolonged turmoil in the Middle East. A global

slowdown due to elevated crude oil prices could weigh on exports.
◆ Reluctance to visit among Chinese travelers is weighing on demand for
travel to Japan
Inbound demand is currently soft. The Chinese government's advisory urging its
citizens to refrain from traveling to Japan is having an adverse impact. The number
of Chinese visitors to Japan has fallen to about half the level prior to the advisory.
Meanwhile, the number of tourists from countries other than China remains firm.

Inbound demand is expected to stay robust going forward. Continued increases in
tourists from countries other than China will remain a supporting factor. The
expectation that the yen will remain weak will also act to push up inbound
consumption. However, there are two downside risks going forward:
The first is that the Chinese government keeps the advisory in place for a
prolonged period. When China banned group travel to South Korea in 2017, tourist

numbers stayed low for two to three years.
The second is the impact of higher crude oil prices. Major airlines have embarked
on phased increases in fuel surcharges, which could weigh on inbound demand
during the summer.

### Page 6

Personal consumption is gradually recovering thanks to a favorable income environment
◆ Higher disposable incomes are supporting personal consumption
Personal consumption continues to gradually recover. While consumption of
non-durable goods remains weak, services consumption is firm, particularly in
amusement-related services. In addition, consumption of durable goods has
increased significantly. Temporary factors such as the abolition of the environmental

performance tax on automobiles (since April 2026) and the tightening of
energy-efficiency standards for air conditioners (effective April 2027) have also
contributed to this rise.
A favorable income environment can also be pointed to as a background factor
behind the recovery in personal consumption. YoY real disposable income growth has
generally been positive since mid-2024, and the pace of increase has recently
accelerated. Employee remuneration is rising, partly as a result of agreements made

during the shunto (spring wage negotiations), and net asset income is also increasing
at a faster clip as companies hike dividends in line with stock price rises.
◆ Stock price developments could be a source of concern for personal
consumption
Personal consumption is expected to continue its gradual recovery going forward. In
addition to the favorable income environment and government policy measures to
curb price increases, a recovery in consumer confidence driven by easing tensions in

the Middle East will also provide a tailwind. Recently, the Consumer Confidence Index
has been showing signs of bottoming out.
However, a reverse wealth effect stemming from falling stock prices is a potential
cause for concern. The Nikkei Stock Average, which reached a new high in June, has
been struggling to push higher since the start of July. Estimates suggest the wealth
effect is concentrated among elderly households, but in recent years working-age

households have also increased their holdings of equity-related assets, and the
positive wealth effect for them may have supported consumption to some extent. For
this reason, if stock prices were to fall significantly, there is a danger of downward
pressure on consumption across a wide range of households.

### Page 7

セン
To
◆ The unemployment rate has settled in the mid-2% range
While the employment situation is generally favorable, weakness can be seen in
some areas. The unemployment rate has remained in the mid-2% range since 2022.
The number of employed persons in non-manufacturing, where business conditions
are favorable, is holding firm, while in manufacturing the number remains at a level
below that seen before the COVID-19 pandemic.
In the background to the drop in manufacturing employment is a slump in

production activity. Manufacturing output has been shrinking due to the effects of
U.S. tariff policy, soaring raw material prices, and a decline in the international
competitiveness of Japanese firms. As a result, pressure to reduce headcounts has
been building. Even in non-manufacturing, some industries closely linked to
manufacturing, such as transportation and wholesale/retail, are also exhibiting
weakness in labor demand.
◆ The employment situation could remain fragile in the near term

Looking ahead at the employment situation in the short term, a sense of
stagnation may persist. The contraction in output in the manufacturing sector will
continue to weigh on labor demand. Although production activity has been brisker
recently, particularly in semiconductor-related industries, the employment-creation
capacity of such industries is small. The employment-inducement coefficient per unit
of new demand tends to be lower than in other sectors, meaning that the industries

lack the strength to serve as drivers of employment.
However, in the medium to long term, the unemployment rate is expected to see a
gradual decline. Demand for labor is projected to stay solid, especially in
face-to-face services such as medical/nursing care and accommodation and
eating/drinking services, but the number of people seeking employment from the
non-labor force population has declined significantly due to the expansion in elderly
and female workforce participation. With limited room for expansion in the labor

supply, the job market is expected to tighten further.

The government is curbing inflation
◆ Policy factors are pushing down the inflation rate
In June, core inflation (all items, less fresh food) was 1.6%, staying under 2%
for the fifth consecutive month. Food price inflation has slowed, and a major

reason for this is a fallback in rice prices, which soared last year. Gasoline
subsidies are also continuing to curtail energy price increases.
Inflation looks set to hover in the 2% range in the second half of 2026. Although
energy subsidies and other government measures will soften inflationary pressure
to some extent, with imported inflationary pressure building, prices are expected
to be pushed up as higher costs are passed through to selling prices. From

FY2027 onward, the reduction in the consumption tax rate for food items will
serve to keep prices down, and inflation is expected to drop into the 1% range.
◆ The Bank of Japan (BOJ) will keep its policy rate unchanged
At its July monetary policy meeting, the BOJ decided to keep its policy rate
unchanged.
Long-term interest rates remain high. This reflects concerns about inflation
resulting from the increase in oil prices and expectations of an expansion in fiscal

expenditure.
The BOJ is expected to step up the pace of interest rate hikes while closely
monitoring the impact of higher rates on the real economy and financial/capital
markets. The next rate hike is expected to be in September.
Long-term interest rates look likely to rise gradually, reflecting heightened
anticipation of rate hikes by the BOJ and increased fiscal spending by the
government. If jitters about a deterioration in fiscal discipline resulting from such

measures as energy subsidies or the consumption tax cut take hold, there is also
a risk that long-term interest rates will rise.

◆ Surging crude oil prices have become a source of upward pressure on
prices
With crude oil prices having spiked against the backdrop of the worsening situation
in the Middle East, corporate input costs have risen sharply, and pressure for price
pass-through is intensifying. Looking at the Final Demand-Intermediate Demand
price indexes, the margin of price increases at the upstream stage far exceeds that at
the downstream stage, and price pass-through pressure is also substantial at the

downstream stage.
According to estimates based on past data, the impact of rising oil prices involves
a time lag. Specifically, the push-up effect on the consumer price inflation rate
increases for around 10 months and continues to exert influence for a long period
thereafter.
◆ Price pass-through willingness is also stronger than before
Notably, food product manufacturers are planning to raise prices for a huge
number of items during the summer and beyond. According to Teikoku Databank, the

number of items slated for price increases as of the end of July, when it conducted its
latest survey, was higher than in the May and June surveys.
If government subsidies for electricity/gas bills and gasoline prices are scaled back
or ended, both energy and food prices will increase concurrently, and the consumer
price inflation rate may rise again.
Attention also needs to be paid to the possibility that price pass-through driven by
elevated oil prices will become prolonged. In the 2020s, the rate of price increases in

transactions between companies has been higher than in the 2010s, and firms'
medium-term outlooks for selling prices have also shifted upward. Events that trigger
cost increases, such as heightened geopolitical tensions, have been occurring
repeatedly, and companies may be more willing to pass through higher costs to
prices than they were before.

### Page 10

The economy is expected to continue to recover, but downside risks are present
◆ The economy is projected to stay on the recovery track
The Japanese economy should continue its gradual recovery. WTI crude oil
futures prices are expected to fall moderately going forward.
In the corporate sector, government support for investment is providing a tailwind,
and demand for investment for labor saving and digital transformation remains
robust. In addition, rising global demand for AI-related goods should support exports.

Supply chain disruptions resulting from the deterioration in the Middle East situation
will begin to ease, and corporate production activity is expected to gradually recover.
In the household sector, inflationary pressure is expected to strengthen from the
summer onward due to higher crude oil prices. In response, the government will
probably ease the inflationary pressure through measures such as energy subsidies.
While the pace of increase in personal consumption is likely to slow somewhat,

consumption is expected to remain resilient amid continued high wage growth. From
FY2027 onward, the reduction in the consumption tax rate for food items should also
support personal consumption.
◆ The Middle East situation and rising interest rates constitute risks
Real GDP growth is projected to remain solid, registering +0.8% in FY2026 and
+0.9% in FY2027.
However, if the situation in the Middle East worsens again or becomes prolonged,
there is a risk that downward pressure on the economy will intensify, mainly through

three channels: 1) weaker exports, 2) raw material supply constraints, and 3) rising
prices. In addition, if market concerns about the fiscal stance of the Takaichi
government grow, the yen could weaken further, and long-term interest rates could
rise, potentially forcing the scaling-back or scrapping of expansionary policies such
as investment support measures, energy subsidies, and lower consumption tax.
Monthly Report of Prospects for Japan's Economy August 2026

The Japan Research Institute, Limited- ８ -

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: WEEKLY WEBCAST: On Inflation, Capital Spending & Economic Resilience
Date:    18 hours ago
WEEKLY WEBCAST: On Inflation, Capital Spending & Economic Resilience

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
WEEKLY WEBCAST: On
Inflation, Capital Spending &
Economic Resilience
By Ed Yardeni • 30 Sept 2026
View in browser

Today, Ed and Elias take a deep dive into core inflation, i.e., minus volatile
food and energy prices. The Fed’s preferred measure, the core PCED,
justifies tightening monetary policy—both the recent September rate hike
and presumably future ones provided that underlying inflation remains
elevated. … The alternative measure, the core CPI, is structured
differently, causing it to diverge from the core PCED in response to price
changes in AI-related spending, financial services, and shelter. … The US

### Page 2

economy should remain resilient during this tightening cycle, as GDP
growth has become increasingly desensitized to interest rates. That’s one
reason the recession widely expected in 2022 and 2023 never happened.

latest webcast. Paid members can enjoy immediate access to the
video. This content will become available to the public at a later
date—don't miss out on early insights, consider upgrading today!

1
《GLP-1公司展望分歧 ，製藥多受惠新藥銷售成長 》
近月GLP-1領域因市場轉關注諾和諾德 (NVO)在巴
西、加拿大等新興市場因 semaglutide專利到期 ，
在糖尿病症恐面臨降價壓力抵銷預期銷量倍數成長 ，
公司雖重申主要市場美國核心專利於 2032年才到期，

減重仍有其他專利保護 ，但計入五款重磅藥物 ，整
體2026–2030年營收複合年增長率(CAGR)僅與大型
製藥同業平均約4-7%年增率相似，使股價大幅下修。
因市場擔憂 GLP-1降價壓力及減重口服藥初期銷量
較慢，近月修正禮來股價估值 ，但七月醫療保險過
渡計畫覆蓋 GLP-1減重藥，已70萬名老年新患者採
用，其中七成為禮來產品 Zepbound，口服新患者
1/3使用禮來Foundayo，預期後續銷售增溫 ，加上
減重自費及國際市場成長 ，市場對禮來仍維持雙位
數成長預估。其他製藥業也因腫瘤藥、免疫等核心
資料來源：JP Morgan，2026/9/18，中國信託商業銀行整理，
2026/9/30
來減重口服藥及針劑穩定成長
• 個別GLP-1因海外專利到期 ，
對長期展望較保守 ，但預期其
他藥廠受惠銷量成長抵銷降價
影響，成長動能仍高 ，其他藥
廠受惠腫瘤 、免疫核心新藥維
持穩定成長。

重點摘要
• 可同時做 消融 +左心 耳封堵術
(LAAC)，但LAAC不能完全替代

健康。

市佔率改善獲利 ，但後續星級
評等 、保費調整能力及理賠成
本控管 ，業者間的長期獲利表
現可能進一步分化。
新藥銷售成長，維持穩定成長，藥廠展望仍佳。
《醫療器械喜憂參半，但必要性手術需求仍佳》
近月醫療服務設備股為健護中股價表現較差 ，主因：
1)亞培受到產品責任險達成部分和解協議 ，後續還
有其他聯邦 、州判決仍為股價壓力； 2)波士頓科學
受資安事件影響生產宣告無法達成財測 ，HRS的左

心耳封堵術 (LAAC) 指南適用 WATCHMAN、 亞培
Amulet，可同時做消融+LAAC，但LAAC不能完全
替代口服抗凝血藥物 (OAC)對預防中風的效用 ，仍
為二線，等同喜憂參半 。另受自付成本增加 ，預期
擇期、外科手術需求疲軟延伸至年底 ，機器人手術
成長率區間接近 14.5%，下半年低於上半年 ，將以
擴大翻新系統的布署降低門檻及增加門診手術需求 ，
擴大目標市場 ，但必要醫療手術需求健康 ，心血管
類手術下半年仍成長，醫療器材基本面喜憂參半。
《星級獎金與醫療成本控管力使長期獲利分化》
每月聯邦醫療保險優勢計畫投保人數成長趨勢持續 ，
CMS預計於2026年10月中公布 2027年醫療優勢計
畫(MA)星級評等，因評級變嚴將影響醫保公司會員
留存率、競爭力及2028年品質獎金收入不確定性增
加，低於4星級業者面臨品質獎金減少與長期獲利承
壓風險。另無意外法案獨立爭議解決 (IDR)仲裁成本
持續推升商業保險理賠支出 ，增加2027年雇主團體
及ACA市場保費定價壓力 。管理式醫療業者今年展

現利潤優先於市占率、砍不賺錢計畫/人數拉抬利潤
率，但明年若保費調漲幅度不及醫療成本增速 ，恐
推升醫療損失率 、壓縮承保利潤 。後續獲利關鍵在
於星級評等 、保費調整能力及理賠成本控管 ，業者
間的長期獲利表現可能進一步分化。

生産
6月の鉱工業生産（関西）は前月比＋1.7%と3ヵ月連続でプラスとなり、持ち直しの動きがみられる。化学、
汎用・生産用・業務用機械が減少したものの、電気機械や電子部品・デバイスが増加した。先行きは、米・イ
ランの対立長期化、中国経済減速、熊本地震の影響などから下振れるリスクがある。

企業景況感（日銀短観）
日銀短観6月調査の業況判断DI（「良い」－「悪い」）は、全産業で＋17ptと3月調査（＋15ｐｔ）から改善。非
製造業は悪化したが、製造業が改善した。業種別に見ると、製造業では、繊維、窯業・土石、鉄鋼などが
改善。非製造業は、建設、不動産、対事業所サービス、対個人サービスなどが悪化した。先行き（9月）に
ついては、製造業、非製造業ともに低下が見込まれている。

Document 390: ドル高・米金利上昇への転換で問われる新興国通貨の耐性

Source PDF: all report pdf/ドル高・米金利上昇への転換で問われる新興国通貨の耐性.pdf
Report source: DIR
Date: 2026-10-05
Pages: 9
Status: ok

### Page 1

新興国

株式会社大和総研 丸の内オフィス　〒100-6756 東京都千代田区丸の内一丁目 9 番 1 号 グラントウキョウ ノースタワー 
このレポートは投資勧誘を意図して提供するものではありません。このレポートの掲載情報は信頼できると考えられる情報源から作成しておりますが、その正確性、完全
性を保証するものではありません。また、記載された意見や予測等は作成時点のものであり今後予告なく変更されることがあります。㈱大和総研と大和証券㈱は、㈱大和

2026 年 10 月 5 日　全 9 頁

ドル高・米金利上昇への転換で問われる新
興国通貨の耐性 
耐性は、メキシコ、マレーシア、ブラジルで比較的高い

経済調査部 シニアエコノミスト 増川 智咲

[要約] 
 2026 年の新興国通貨は、中東危機を背景とする原油高とリスク回避姿勢の強まり、米
国の実質金利上昇に伴う対米金利差の縮小という共通のショックを受けたが、通貨間
のパフォーマンスには差があった。 
 2026年4～6月には、多くの国で交易条件や輸出入数量の動向、サービス・所得収支を
含む経常収支の構造が、通貨の強弱と概ね整合した。ただし、インド、南アフリカ
（南ア） 、トルコでは、経常収支だけでは為替動向を説明できず、資金フローやインフ
レ期待などの金融面の影響を大きく受けた。また、2026 年 7 月以降は、ドル安に加え、
各国の利上げや為替介入等が通貨を下支えしてきた。 
 9 月 16 日の米国の利上げと追加利上げ観測は、新興国通貨にとって再びリスクとなり
うる。今後の耐性を総合すると、メキシコ、マレーシア、ブラジルは、経常収支、外
貨獲得力、直接投資の規模、外貨準備の厚みなどの面で相対的に下支え材料が多い。
一方、インドネシアとフィリピンは厚い外貨準備を有するものの、経常赤字と市場性
資金の変動に注意が必要である。タイはエネルギー高に対して脆弱ではあるが、外貨
獲得力や直接投資、外貨準備の厚みが緩衝材になる見通しである。対象国の中では、
海外借入依存度の高さ、期待インフレの高さ、低い政策信認という点で、トルコの脆
弱性が最も大きい。

### Page 2

1

2 / 9

2026 年の新興国通貨：共通ショックの後に鮮明となった国別格差 
2026 年の新興国通貨を取り巻く主なリスクには、中東危機を背景とする原油高とリスク回避
姿勢の強まり、米国の実質金利上昇に伴う対米金利差の縮小、ドル高への転換などが挙げられ
る。ただし、各要因の強さは時期によって大きく変化した。このため、本稿では新興国通貨の
対ドル為替レートの動向を 3 つの局面に分けて整理する（図表 1） 。 
第 1 の局面は、中東危機が生じる前の①2025 年 12 月末～2026 年 2 月 28 日である。この時期
は、米連邦準備制度理事会（FRB）の独立性をめぐる懸念や利下げ期待などを背景にドル安が
進み、多くの新興国通貨が対ドルで上昇した。 
第2の局面は、②米国によるイラン攻撃から、4月8日の米国・イラン間の初回停戦発表まで
である。中東情勢の緊迫化に伴う原油価格の上昇と、金融市場におけるリスク回避姿勢の強ま
りを受け、新興国通貨は対ドルで幅広く下落した。 
第 3 の局面は、③初回停戦発表から FRB による利上げ直前（2026 年 9 月 11 日）までである。
ただし、この期間も中東情勢は完全には安定せず、為替市場を動かす要因は変化した。 
本稿では、第 3 局面を 4～6 月期の③-1 と、7 月から FRB 利上げ直前の 9 月 11 日までの③-2
とし、前者については貿易・経常収支、後者についてはドル相場、金利差、資本フロー、政策
対応を軸として検討する。 
図表 1 新興国通貨の対ドル為替レート変化率と米ドル実効レートの変化率（2026 年の期間別）

（注 1）南アは、南アフリカ。 
（注 2）米ドルは、プラスが実効レートの上昇を表す。 
（出所）Bloomberg、Wall Street Journal/Haver Analytics より大和総研作成

### Page 3

1

3 / 9

通貨間格差の要因①～貿易・経常収支を通じた影響 
まず、③-1 にあたる 2026 年 4～6 月期について、通貨パフォーマンスの差を貿易・経常収支
の面から確認する。図表2～4は、各国の経常収支と貿易収支の変動要因を示している。資源価
格の上昇などに伴う交易条件の変化だけでなく、輸出入数量、サービス収支、所得収支も併せ
て確認する。

経常収支悪化と通貨安が整合的だったタイ、インドネシア、フィリピン 
③-1 の期間に対ドルで為替レートが下落した国のうち、タイ、インドネシア、フィリピンで
は、2026 年に入って経常赤字（GDP 比）が拡大した（図表 2） 。経常赤字の拡大は、これらの通
貨が軟調に推移したことと整合的である。 
タイとフィリピンでは、貿易赤字の拡大が経常収支悪化の主因となった。背景には、エネル
ギー価格上昇による交易条件の悪化と、輸入数量の増加がある（図表 3、4） 。両国は、本稿の
対象国の中でもエネルギー輸入への依存度が高い。加えて、AI ブームを背景に電子機器などの
輸出向け生産が拡大し、中間財輸入が増加した可能性がある。ただし、輸出向け中間財の輸入
増は、将来の生産・輸出拡大に伴う側面もあるため、外部脆弱性の悪化と一概に評価できない
点には留意が必要である。 
インドネシアでは、原油高の局面においても貿易黒字を維持したが、黒字幅が縮小し、経常
赤字の拡大につながった。貿易黒字縮小の原因には、交易条件の悪化に加え、輸出数量の減少
があった。同国の輸出構造は資源の割合が比較的高く、マレーシアに比べて AI関連の電子機器
輸出拡大の恩恵を受けにくい。最大の輸出先である中国の内需減速も、輸出の下押し要因とな
ったとみられる。

経常収支だけでは通貨安を説明しにくいインドとトルコ 
経常収支の動向だけで通貨安を十分に説明できない国がインドとトルコである。インドはエ
ネルギーの純輸入国であり、2026 年 4～6 月期の財の貿易赤字の規模は GDP 比 10％近くにまで
拡大した。一方、IT・ビジネスサービスを中心とするサービス収支と、海外送金を中心とする
第二次所得収支の黒字が財の貿易赤字の相当分を相殺し、経常赤字は比較的小幅にとどまった。 
トルコもエネルギーの純輸入国であることから、2026年4～6月期は交易条件が悪化した。た
だし、高金利による需要抑制が消費財輸入を抑え、貿易赤字と経常赤字はいずれも前期比で縮
小した。それにもかかわらず、対象国の中でリラの下落率が最も大きかったことは、経常取引
以外の要因が強く作用していたことを示唆している。

### Page 4

1

4 / 9

経常取引が通貨の下支えとなったマレーシアとメキシコ 
マレーシアとメキシコでは、良好な経常取引が通貨の下支えとなったとみられる。両国は
2026年4～6月期に貿易黒字・経常黒字を維持した。マレーシアでは天然ガス価格の上昇に伴う
交易条件の改善に加え、半導体・電子機器などの輸出数量の増加が寄与した。同国の貿易黒字
は GDP 比 7.5％に達し、経常黒字を支えた。 
メキシコでは、米国向けを中心とする工業品輸出の増加が貿易黒字に大きく寄与した。ニア
ショアリングを背景とする生産拠点の集積も、輸出基盤を支えているとみられる。両国では、
堅調な財の輸出を背景とした外貨獲得力が、通貨の下支え材料となった。

経常赤字の「質」が異なるブラジルと、経常取引だけで通貨高を説明できない南ア 
ブラジルでは、資源価格の上昇に伴う交易条件の改善により、2026年4～6月期の貿易黒字幅
は前期から拡大した。一方、外国企業への配当支払いや国債の利子払い等による第一次所得収
支の大幅な赤字を背景に、経常収支は赤字となった。ブラジルの経常赤字は、輸出競争力の低
下やエネルギー輸入の増加に起因するものではない。この点で、貿易赤字の拡大を伴う国と比
べて、対外収支面の脆弱性は相対的に小さい。 
南アフリカ（南ア）では、原油高による交易条件の悪化を受け、2026年4～6月期の貿易黒字
幅が前期から大きく縮小した。さらに、第一次所得収支の赤字規模が大きいことから、経常収
支は GDP比▲0.9％の赤字となった。経常収支が悪化した一方、ランドが対ドルで上昇した背景
には、資本フローなどの経常取引以外の要因が考えられる。 
このように、③-1 では、多くの国で、貿易・経常収支の動向が通貨パフォーマンスの差と一
定程度整合した。米国の金利が相対的に安定していて金利差による変動が目立ちにくかったこ
とに加え、エネルギー高や AIブームといった貿易・経常収支要因が主な為替変動要因だったた
めだと考えられる。 
例外は、インド、南ア、トルコである。 3 カ国では、経常収支と通貨パフォーマンスが必ず
しも一致しなかった。また 7月以降である③-2の期間になると、③-1に経常収支が悪化してい
たタイとインドネシアで為替レートが対ドルで上昇に転じた。つまり、③全体の通貨動向を説
明するには、経常取引だけでなく、金利差を意識した資本フローや政策対応など、金融面の要
因による影響が大きい可能性があるため、次節で考察する。

### Page 5

1

5 / 9

図表 2 新興国の経常収支

（注 1）GDP は3 四半期の平均を用いた。Q4 は 10-12 月期、Q1 は 1-3 月期、Q2 は 4-6 月期。 
（注 2）タイの2026 年 Q2 のデータは貿易・経常収支以外未公表。 
（注 3）国際収支は非季節調整値。 
（出所）各国統計より大和総研作成

図表 3　貿易収支の要因分解（前期との差）

（注）季節調整値を使用。 
（出所）各国統計より大和総研作成 
 図表 4　貿易収支の要因分解（前期との差）

（注）季節調整値を使用。 
（出所）各国統計より大和総研作成

通貨間格差の要因②～資本フロー・金利差の影響 
2026 年 4～6 月期（③-1）に、経常収支と為替が乖離したトルコ、インド、南ア 
③-1 に経常取引要因だけでは為替動向を説明しにくかった、トルコ、インド、南アについて、
